Idempotent. **All current migrations are already applied to the live DB** (verified May 2026): `001_units_whale_tier.sql` (added 10u + whale confidence), `002_to_win_pnl.sql` (recompute pnl_units to to-WIN model), `003_resolver_heartbeat.sql` (resolver_runs heartbeat table — read by `/api/handicapper`), `004`–`006` (legacy Polymarket-link columns — Pick Bot no longer auto-links, see gotcha #26), `007_clv.sql` (added `clv_pp` — Closing Line Value per pick, computed by the resolver from PIN's closing line in `book_snapshots`). `008_nrfi.sql` (NRFI/YRFI — adds market_type `'nrfi'` + side `'yes'`/`'no'` to the CHECK constraints; **applied to the live DB May 2026** via `run_sql.sh`). `009_half_unit.sql` (widen `units` integer→numeric + add 0.5 to the CHECK; **applied**). `010_quarter_unit.sql` (add 0.25 to the CHECK — NRFI dropped 0.5u→0.25u; keeps 0.5 so historical 0.5u rows still settle; **applied to the live DB**). `015_prop.sql` (add `'prop'` to the market_type CHECK — generic exchange props, side yes/no; **applied July 26 2026**; see "Prop Bets pipeline"). `017_poly_pnl.sql` (dedicated `poly_pnl` jsonb column for the MONEY LEDGER — `app.py:_poly_ledger_tick`, every 5th paperlog tick, stamps venue cash truth per (pmm_slug, leg) from the Polymarket activities feed: buys/sells/resolution payouts, `realized_usd` final when flat/resolved. TWO LEDGERS BY DESIGN: ESPN-graded units = the MODEL's scoreboard (shadows have no venue position); `poly_pnl` = the ACCOUNT's scoreboard, the only one that can see a SELL — the prerequisite for any trading/exit lane. Own column because signal_blob has many full-blob read-modify-write writers and the last one silently dropped ledger stamps; **applied Aug 3 2026**. Parsing landmines survived: TRADE activities carry NULL netPositions → sell-detection is `realizedPnl non-null OR position shrank`, leg from position sign else metadata outcome else merged into the slug's sole picked leg). `016_autolog_uniq.sql` (partial UNIQUE index on autolog-created rows — the autolog's check-then-insert dedup raced across its two callers (cron reconcile + fill-status compute) and double-logged bets 47ms apart (the "Padres bet doubled" incident); the index makes the losing racer's insert fail into the existing try/except. Scoped to `signal_blob->>'source' in ('pmm_autolog','kalshi_autolog')` ONLY — user-logged picks legitimately repeat on that key (upgrade DELTA rows, multiple props per game); **applied July 26 2026**).
- `kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper.py` — Pick Bot dossier builder (free public data + Supabase)
- `kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper_log_pick.py` — Pick Bot pick logger (writes to `bot_picks`)
- `kahla-scanner/scripts/bot_picks_resolver.py` — Pick Bot resolver. Runs every cron-job.org tick (1 min as of May 2026; was 30 min) as the last step of `scanner-poll.yml`. Idempotent — already-graded rows are skipped, so 1-min cadence just means faster pickup of newly-completed games. Grades pending `bot_picks` whose `event_start` has passed via ESPN final scores. UFC ML graded via ESPN MMA endpoint (`mma/ufc`). UFC SPR/TOT method-of-victory bets stay pending — user settles them via the page. Writes a heartbeat row to `resolver_runs` on every run (success or crash) so the `/handicapper` header can show "graded Nm ago" or red CRASHED with full traceback. PnL is to-WIN sizing — kept in sync with `app.py`'s manual-settle endpoint. Also computes **Closing Line Value** (`clv_pp`) per pick at grade time via `_compute_clv` (the **exchange** pre-`event_start` closing pair via `_exch_close_pair` — Kalshi mid for ML, PMM for SPR/TOT, devigged — vs the entry price; PIN `book_snapshots` only as a warm fallback, June 2026 cutover) — written once, never recomputed.
- `kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper_backtest.py` — Pick Bot backtest replay (rule-based, signals-only). PIN-only — stale post-cutover (the dossier-mirror rule is moot; `handicapper_web.py` is the only live dossier).
- `kahla-scanner/scripts/tune_prime_window.py` — **weekly prime-window auto-tuner** (the agentic self-adjust). The prime window is **MULTI-ZONE** (the edge is bimodal — see below). Bins settled gate-cleared ML/SPR/TOT `pickbot_paperlog` rows into 30-min bands, marks a band GOOD on enough sample + positive mean per-pick **unit-ROI** (sizing-neutral → isolates the TIMING edge), merges consecutive good bands into zones, drops thin zones + ones failing the **CLV guardrail**. Writes the zone list to `pickbot_tuning.zones` (jsonb `[[lo,hi],…]`) + the `(prime_lo,prime_hi)` envelope; `handicapper_web._load_prime_zones` reads it at runtime (5-min cache, `_PRIME_ZONES` constant fallback) and `_timing_window` classifies prime/late/far/**gap** (the hole between zones → capped 1u, still bet if the gate clears). **Current live zones (July 3 2026, manual retune on the DEDUPED paperlog — the June-29 tuner run had ingested the dup flood, which inflated the pooled zone to a bogus `[[0,180]]`): pooled `zones` (SPR fallback) = `[[30,90]]`; `zones_by_market.moneyline` = `[[30,180]]` (one continuous zone — 0-30min is a losing band, 30min-3h continuously positive); `total` = `[[30,60],[150,180]]`; `nrfi` = `[[0,30],[150,210]]`.** Recomputed with the tuner's own rules via SQL (basis JSON on row id=1 documents it); the weekly run recomputes from clean data (and now EXCLUDES shadow spread-model rows). **REPORTING CONVENTION — zones are stored in MINUTES-before-first-pitch (`[lo,hi]`); when telling the user their prime windows, ALWAYS convert to `Xh Ym` to match the website's "starts in 1h 55m" countdown, NOT raw minutes** (e.g. 150→2h 30m, 120→2h, 90→1h 30m, 60→1h, 30→30m). To answer "what are my prime windows" pull the latest `pickbot_tuning` row via `run_sql.sh` (it's per-market: check both `zones` and `zones_by_market`) and translate. Plumbed to `/handicapper` via `/api/handicapper/games`'s `prime_zones` so the row glow follows the tuner without hardcoding. Run via `.github/workflows/tune-prime-window.yml` (weekly Mon 12:00 UTC + `workflow_dispatch` dry-run/lookback). DDL: `kahla-scanner/supabase/pickbot_tuning.sql` + `pickbot_tuning_zones.sql` (both applied). Safe by construction — leaves zones alone on thin data. **Sandbox note:** can't run the script here (supabase py client hits the cffi/crypto load bug) — validate the metric via `run_sql.sh` SQL instead.
- `kahla-scanner/scripts/nrfi_backtest.py` — NRFI/YRFI first-inning model calibration backtest (MLB Stats API, no secrets; `.github/workflows/nrfi-backtest.yml` runs it). Mirrors `handicapper_web.py`'s `NRFI_*` constants — keep in sync.
- **Power-ratings pipeline (the real OUR-NUMBER engine — see "Power-ratings pipeline" section):**
  - `kahla-scanner/_lib/power_ratings.py` — the engine: iterative opponent-adjusted off/def/net (SRS variant), recency half-life decay, `project()` + `margin_to_prob()` + `calibrate()` (fits HFA + logistic scale from results). Pure Python. Runs in the cron only (Flask can't import the kahla-scanner subproject).
  - `kahla-scanner/scripts/ingest_results.py` — pulls ESPN finals into `game_results` (`--days N` backfills). Idempotent upsert.
  - `kahla-scanner/scripts/compute_power_ratings.py` — reads the finals window per sport, runs the engine + calibration, writes one `power_ratings` snapshot row per sport.
  - `kahla-scanner/scripts/backtest_power_ratings.py` — walk-forward backtest harness (per-sport accuracy/Brier/calibration). Validates the TEAM core only (not the MLB pitcher layer, not live CLV).
  - `kahla-scanner/supabase/power_ratings.sql` — DDL for `game_results` + `power_ratings`. Must exist before the pipeline works (else Flask silently uses the v1 season-stat fallback). **Already applied to the live DB** (verified May 2026 via `run_sql.sh`); re-run with `run_sql.sh -f` if you ever rebuild the project.
  - `.github/workflows/power-ratings.yml` — daily 11:00 UTC: `ingest_results` + `compute_power_ratings`; `workflow_dispatch` with `days` input backfills a season. Separate from `scanner-poll.yml`.
  - `.github/workflows/power-ratings-backtest.yml` — manual `workflow_dispatch`; prints walk-forward metrics to the run log.
  - The Flask-side projection lives in `handicapper_web.py:_power_rating_v2` (reads the latest `power_ratings` snapshot, runs the lightweight off/def→margin projection + all the per-game layers: pitcher, bullpen, park, lineup, goalie, injuries, rest). `_power_rating_v1` is the raw-season-stat fallback.
- `kahla-scanner/supabase/bot_picks.sql` — `bot_picks` table DDL (run via `run_sql.sh -f` or the Supabase SQL editor; **already applied** as of May 2026)
- `kahla-scanner/supabase/odds_ingest_runs.sql` — heartbeat table for the adaptive-cadence ingest gate. One row per cron tick per sport, records fire/skip decisions for observability. Run via `run_sql.sh -f` or the Supabase SQL editor (**already applied** as of May 2026).
- `kahla-scanner/scrapers/odds_api.py` — The Odds API ingester (cron entry point)
- `kahla-scanner/scripts/cleanup_snapshots.py` — nightly book_snapshots > 15d delete
- `kahla-scanner/scripts/sharp_alerts.py` — Steam detection + steam paper-bet logger. Telegram alerts retired May 2026 (too noisy); the script itself runs every cron-job.org tick (1 min) in `scanner-poll.yml` with `STEAM_SILENT=1`, which short-circuits `_telegram_send()` to a no-op success. Steam dedup is keyed on `(market_id, market_type, alert_type, side)` with a 24h window, so 1-min cadence doesn't flood paper_bets — just catches steam faster. End result: detection + dedup + paper-bet row insert all run; nothing pings the phone. UFC markets blocked via `pb.BLOCKED_SPORTS`. The `⚡ SHARP N` (sharp7) path is also short-circuited under STEAM_SILENT — its alerts never wrote paper-bets in the first place, so silent mode just makes it a no-op detection log. If you ever want the Telegram messages back, unset `STEAM_SILENT` and provide `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` / `TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID`. (Sharp-score math still uses local copies of the helpers in this file — keep them in sync with `kahla-scanner/_lib/sharp.py`.)
- `kahla-scanner/scripts/paper_bets_picker.py` — Phase 4 Early/Late EV pickers. `--bot early` runs 1×/day via `paper-bets-early.yml`; `--bot late` runs every cron-job.org tick (1 min) appended to `scanner-poll.yml`. Per-`(market_id, bot)` 7-day dedup means late picks don't get re-logged on every tick — once a game qualifies and is picked, it's locked for that bot until 7 days pass.
- `kahla-scanner/_lib/sharp.py` — sharp-score math + sharp-side detection. Used by the paper-bet picker. (`sharp_alerts.py` still has local copies of these helpers — DRY violation kept on purpose to minimise blast radius on the live alert pipeline; both implementations are bytewise identical and any future fix lands in both.)
- `kahla-scanner/_lib/paper_bets.py` — paper-bet shared helpers: PIN devig, best-entry finder, score formula, dedup check, insert helper, snapshot loaders.
- `kahla-scanner/supabase/paper_bets.sql` — `paper_bets` table DDL. Run manually in Supabase SQL editor.
- `kahla-scanner/storage/{models,supabase_client}.py` — slim Supabase wrapper
- `kahla-scanner/_lib/{matcher,normalize}.py` — team-name fuzzy match + odds math
- `templates/book_club.html` — **Book Club page**: self-contained (embedded CSS/JS, Firebase auth + `authFetch` + `/api/me` gate, same pattern as `handicapper.html`). Five sections: Currently Reading, **Find a Meeting Day** (day-level availability calendar + suggestion engine), Next-Read Vote, Meeting Schedule, Reading List. All data via the `/api/book-club*` endpoints — no Supabase, no client-side Firestore SDK writes (unlike `index.html`, which writes the `users` collection directly). Availability is day-level only (members mark whole days off; no time-of-day); the suggester lists days nobody marked off and "Set as meeting" (manager-only) pins the current book to that day at 5 PM. **Book add/edit/delete + Set-as-meeting are gated to club managers** (`_canManage` client-side, `_is_club_manager()` server-side); voting, rating, and marking availability stay open to all members. **Branding**: the club logo lives at `static/well-red-logo.png`, served via the Flask route `/book-club-logo.png` (`send_file`, ships on disk like templates — there's no Vercel static handler, so it's an explicit route). Used as the `/book-club` page hero, its favicon/home-screen icon, and the home-page app-card icon. The whole `/book-club` page is themed **wine + cream** to match the logo (its `:root` retints `--cyan`→wine `#c14a5e` as the primary accent, `--green`→sage for "everyone free", warm dark base, gold ratings) — scoped to that page only; the rest of the Kahla House platform keeps its blue/teal theme. A subtle wine-burgundy ambient glow (`body::after`) sits behind the hero.
- `firestore.rules` — Firestore security rules (admin/approved helpers). **Not touched by Book Club** — its collections (`book_club_books` / `book_club_polls` / `book_club_availability`) are written ONLY by the Flask Admin SDK (which bypasses rules), so they need no client rules; the `book_club_access` field on user docs is already covered by `allow update: if isAdmin()`.
- `vercel.json` — Vercel deployment config
- `requirements.txt` — Python deps (flask, polymarket-us, requests, python-dotenv, firebase-admin, supabase, **beautifulsoup4**, **lxml**)
- `.env` — Local env vars (DO NOT COMMIT — contains API keys)

## Environment Variables

| Variable | Where | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `ODDS_API_KEY` | GitHub Actions secret + Vercel env | The Odds API key (100K-credit tier, $59/mo) |
| `POLYMARKET_KEY_ID` | Vercel env | Polymarket US API key ID |
| `POLYMARKET_SECRET_KEY` | Vercel env | Polymarket US API secret |
| `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` | Vercel env | Firebase Admin SDK service account JSON |
| `FLASK_SECRET_KEY` | Vercel env | Flask session secret |
| `SUPABASE_URL` | GitHub Actions secret + Vercel env | Supabase Postgres URL |
| `SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY` | GitHub Actions secret + Vercel env | Supabase service key |
| `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN` | Claude Code cloud env var (optional) | Account-level personal token (`sbp_…`) that lets `kahla-scanner/scripts/run_sql.sh` run SQL/migrations from a Claude Code session via the Management API. NOT used by Flask or the cron. Stored plaintext in the env config (no secrets store) — fine for a solo account; revocable in Supabase → Account → Access Tokens. Pair with `SUPABASE_PROJECT_REF` + allowlist `api.supabase.com`. |
| `SUPABASE_PROJECT_REF` | Claude Code cloud env var (optional) | The project subdomain (the `xxxx` in `https://xxxx.supabase.co`). Used with `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN` by `run_sql.sh`. |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` | GitHub Actions secret — **UNUSED as of May 2026** | Was used by the retired `scripts/sharp_alerts.py`. Safe to delete from GitHub secrets; nothing reads it anymore. |
| `TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID` | GitHub Actions secret — **UNUSED as of May 2026** | Was used by the retired `scripts/sharp_alerts.py`. Safe to delete from GitHub secrets. |
| `FILLED_BOT_TOKEN` | Vercel env | Telegram bot token for the **Filled Bot**. Jobs riding the paperlog tick: the **unlogged-BET alert** (`_bet_alerts`), the **OUTBID push** (`_outbid_alerts` — the red chip's Telegram twin: a resting maker order lost the touch; one ping per public-bid LEVEL via `signal_blob.outbid_warned.best_bid_c` — a HIGHER bid re-pings, the same level never), autobet/repeg notices. **ALL messages are BATCHED (Aug 2 2026, user: max one summary per 10 min — "nothing happened → no message"):** `_send_fill_telegram(text, urgent=False)` queues to the `telegram_queue` table; `_tg_flush` sends ONE 📬 digest per `_TG_BATCH_MIN`=10 min (3600-char budget, 3-day retention; DDL `kahla-scanner/supabase/telegram_queue.sql`, applied). `urgent=True` (🚨-class: ORDER LOST, canceled-order limbo) bypasses the queue and sends immediately via `_tg_send_now`. The TAKE-WARNING push was killed the same night. Re-peg STOP pings fire once per BET (`signal_blob.repeg_stop` marker, re-armed by a successful move — not once per tick). |
| `FILLED_BOT_CHAT_ID` | Vercel env | Your Telegram user id (numeric) — the Filled Bot's recipient. |
| `FILLS_CRON_SECRET` | Vercel env | Random shared secret (`openssl rand -hex 32`) accepted as the `?key=` fallback by every cron-pinged endpoint (pm-snapshot, paperlog, vsin-snapshot, ticker). Named for the retired check-fills endpoint; kept because it's plumbed through cron-job.org + the `fill_ping` workflow job + the widget. |
| `KALSHI_API_KEY_ID` | Vercel env | Kalshi API key id (account settings → API keys) for the AUTHED portfolio reader (fill status). Legacy spellings also scanned (`KALSHI_ACCESS_KEY`/`KALSHI_KEY_ID`/`KALSHI_API_KEY`) — `/api/kalshi/probe` reports which name was found. Absent → fill status quietly no-ops. |
| `KALSHI_PRIVATE_KEY` | Vercel env | The RSA private key (PEM) paired with the key id — literal `\n` in the env value is normalized. Legacy spellings scanned (`KALSHI_API_PRIVATE_KEY`/`KALSHI_RSA_PRIVATE_KEY`). Signing = RSA-PSS(SHA256) over `ts+METHOD+path`. |
| `PARLAY_API_KEY` | Vercel env (optional) | parlay-api.com key (free 1K credits/mo) for the **bet-time Pinnacle stamp** (see the paperlog section). Falls back to the `parlay_state` DB row `api_key` (where it currently lives — user's call); absent both → silent no-op. |
| `RESEND_API_KEY` | Vercel env | [Resend](https://resend.com) API key for **Book Club meeting invites**. When unset, the invite send no-ops (returns `reason:"email_not_configured"`; the book still saves). |
| `BOOK_CLUB_FROM_EMAIL` | Vercel env (optional) | Invite "from" address. Defaults to `{BOOK_CLUB_NAME} <bookclub@thekahlahouse.com>`. The sender domain (`thekahlahouse.com`) must be **verified in Resend** or sends fail; for quick testing use Resend's `onboarding@resend.dev`. |
| `BOOK_CLUB_NAME` | Vercel env (optional) | Display name of the club in invite emails (subject/header/footer + default sender name). Code default is **`Well Red Book Club`** (`app.py` constant). Override here to rename without a code change (redeploy required). |

---

## Odds Board (`/odds`) — RETIRED (June 2026)

Ran on Pinnacle/`book_snapshots`, which froze at the exchange cutover, so it was retired rather than rebuilt. **Removed:** `templates/odds.html`, the odds-only endpoints (`/api/odds`, `/api/odds/history`, `/api/odds/history-batch`, `/api/openers*`) + the openers helpers, `kahla-scanner/scrapers/odds_api.py`, the landing card + nav links; `/odds` now redirects home. **Kept (shared, do not remove):** `/api/splits` + `_fetch_action_splits` (Pick Bot dossier), `_merge_espn_scores`/ESPN helpers, `book_snapshots` + `odds_ingest_runs` tables (Pick Bot/resolver PIN+CLV warm fallback), the snapshot-cleanup workflow, `_fetch_odds_from_snapshots` + `/debug-snap`. Full spec in git history.

## Dashboard (`/dashboard`)

### Features
- **Stats cards**: Balance, Open Positions, Portfolio Value, Today's P&L, Yesterday's P&L, Maker Rewards, Total P&L, Win Rate
- **Open Positions table**: Market, Pick, Qty, Entry, Current, P&L, Return %
- **Closed Positions tab**: Resolved bets + sold trades + maker rewards with Result (W/L/Sold/Maker) and P&L
- **Maker Rewards**: income from Polymarket, counted in P&L + the Maker Rewards stat card + closed positions. **Polymarket renamed these activity types May 2026** — the old single `ACTIVITY_TYPE_TRANSFER` was split into `ACTIVITY_TYPE_LIQUIDITY_PROGRAM` (maker / liquidity-provider rewards — the bulk of it) and `ACTIVITY_TYPE_TAKER_FEE_REBATE` (small fee rebates). `parse_activities` handles all three (their payload nests under the `accountBalanceChange` key); the `_REWARD_TYPE_LABELS` set (`{"Transfer", "Liquidity Program", "Taker Fee Rebate"}`) drives both `compute_summary`'s `is_maker` rollup and the `closed_positions` filter in `api_data`. Closed-positions badges: "Maker" for Transfer/Liquidity Program, "Rebate" for Taker Fee Rebate. `ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_DEPOSIT` = user cash funding (NOT P&L — explicitly `pnl=None` so it never counts as income). `ACTIVITY_TYPE_ACCOUNT_WITHDRAWAL` = withdrawals (NOT P&L). See gotcha #36.
- **Bet Slip modal**: Shareable sportsbook-ticket format. Three sections in display order: **Open Orders** (unfilled limit orders from `/api/my-orders` — forward-looking "here's what I'm trying to get into"; shows fill progress like `1/100` for partials), **Pending** (held positions awaiting outcome — from `/api/data`), **Settled Today** (resolved-today bets with W/L/Sold/Maker badges). Orders intentionally don't show in the Open Positions or Closed Positions tabs — they're only on the betslip because they represent intent, not active risk. **Share button** (top-right of header) rasterizes the entire slip — including off-viewport content — to PNG via [html2canvas](https://html2canvas.hertzen.com/) (CDN); on mobile it hands the image to `navigator.share()` (pops the iMessage / share sheet) with the auto-text "Another day of heartbreak and losses queued up!", on desktop it downloads as `kahla-house-betslip-YYYY-MM-DD.png`. Capture forces `max-height: none` on `.betslip.capturing` so the image grabs the full content even if the on-screen modal is scrolled.
- **CLV column on Open Positions** + **Avg CLV stat card**: per-position Closing Line Value (vs PIN's devigged closing line). Bets whose game hasn't started yet show `--` (no closing line yet). Stat card averages all matched positions; rolls in/out as games start/finish. Bets we can't match (non-sport markets, slug parse failures) just don't appear in the rolled-up average — silent skip. See `/api/clv` route + `_clv_extract_match_info` / `_clv_find_market` / `_clv_pin_close_pair` helpers in `app.py`.
- **Auto-refresh**: 60 seconds (loads `/api/data`, `/api/my-orders`, and `/api/clv` in parallel)

### P&L Computation — CRITICAL NOTES
- **Do NOT trust SDK's `price` field** — it returns the COMPLEMENT (YES price when trading NO, vice versa). Always use `cost / qty` for actual per-share price paid or received
- **Do NOT trust SDK's `realizedPnl` value** — it uses complement pricing. Only use non-null as a sell indicator
- **Sell detection**: `realizedPnl is not None` (primary) or `beforePosition.netPosition > afterPosition.netPosition` (fallback)
- **Trade P&L formula**: `(sell_cost/sell_qty - avg_buy_cost_per_share) * sell_qty`
- Self-tracking average cost: accumulate buy `cost` values per slug (NOT `price`), compute avg cost per share
- Both "Position Resolution" AND closed trades count toward realized P&L, win rate, daily P&L
- Activity cutoff: filters out activity before `2026-03-01`
- **SDK fields on trades**:
  - `price` — COMPLEMENT, do not use for P&L (e.g., reports 0.76 when you paid 0.25/share)
  - `cost` — actual dollars spent (buy) or received (sell). Use `cost.value / qty` for real per-share price
  - `qty` — number of shares
  - `realizedPnl` — unreliable value, but non-null = sell indicator
  - `costBasis` — original cost basis (on sells)
  - `originalPrice` — original entry price (on sells)
  - `beforePosition` / `afterPosition` — position state before/after trade (netPosition, cost fields may be null)

## Polymarket Market Lookup (Pick Bot)

For each Pick Bot dossier, we look up the matching Polymarket event and pull its ML / Spread / Total markets with live bid/ask, so the suggestion card can target PMM's actual line at a PMM-equivalent fair price (push-rate-adjusted from PIN's devigged fair). Implementation in `pmm_markets.py` + `pmm_push_rates.py`.

### Flow

1. Dossier resolves game → `(sport, away, home, event_start_iso)`.
2. `pmm_markets.lookup(client, sport, away, home, event_start_iso)`:
   - Calls `client.events.list(tagSlug=<sport-slug>, startTimeMin=event_start-12h, startTimeMax=event_start+12h, closed=false, limit=100)`. Tries 3 param shapes in sequence (tag+time, tag+relatedTags+time, tag-only) — first non-empty response wins. NOTE: `active=true` is intentionally NOT set — PMM events aren't flagged active until close to tip, and that filter silently excluded same-day games.
   - Matches by team name via `_match_event_to_game`: full-name two-way substring → last-token (e.g. "orioles"+"rays") → market-team fallback. If filter search misses, falls back to `client.search.query(query="<away> <home>")`.
   - `events.list` returns event metadata with an **EMPTY markets array**. After matching, fetch the actual markets via `client.markets.list({eventSlug:[slug], closed:false})` (primary) or `events.retrieve_by_slug` (fallback).
   - Classifies each market via `sportsMarketTypeV2` (`_MONEYLINE`/`_SPREAD`/`_TOTAL`) + filters prop variants via the `sportsMarketType` v1 allowlist. Side from `_side_by_first_mention` on the `question` text — which matches each team by full name, **city/region**, OR mascot last-token (`_team_mention_pos`). The city variant is load-bearing: PMM titles many NBA/NFL markets by city ("Spread: Oklahoma City (-3)", "Oklahoma City to win") where neither "oklahoma city thunder" nor "thunder" appears, so without it ML/spread markets fail side detection and get silently dropped while totals (which default to OVER and need no side) survive — the exact `ml:0 / spread:0 / total:14` symptom (see gotcha #37). Line from the `line` field.
   - Reads bid/ask from the market's embedded `bestBidQuote`/`bestAskQuote` (NO separate bbo() call). Each PMM market is one binary YES/NO; the opposite side is synthesized via `_inverse_quote` (NO bid = 1−YES ask) + `_inverse_side` (spread line sign-flips, total line unchanged).
3. `_attach_pmm_to_odds()` in `handicapper_web.py` walks the structured PMM result and attaches a `polymarket` block onto each `odds[market_type]` block — per-side line, slug, quote (bid/ask/mid), and projected fair (PIN's devig pushed to PMM's line via push rate). `best_line_for` picks the PMM entry closest to PIN's line per side.
4. `_suggest_picks` prefers the projected fair as `fair_american` when projection is applicable; falls back to PIN raw fair otherwise (e.g., PMM line >0.5 pts from PIN — push-rate projection isn't reliable beyond one half-point).

### Push-rate tables (`pmm_push_rates.py`)

| Sport | Market | Key line(s) | Push rate | Other lines |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| NFL | spread | 3 | 9.5% | 1.5% default |
| NFL | spread | 7 | 5.5% | |
| NFL | total | 41, 44, 47 | ~3% | 1.5% default |
| NBA | spread | 3 | 4% | 2.5% default |
| NBA | total | 200, 210, 220 | 3% | 2.5% default |
| NHL | total | 5 | 6% | 2% default |
| NHL | total | 6 | 5% | |
| MLB | total | 8 | 3% | 2% default |
| CBB | spread | 3 | 4.5% | 2.5% default |
| NCAAF | spread | 3 | 7.5% | 1.5% default |

Sources: Stanford Wong's "Sharper" + Sports Insights archives + BoydsBets. Numbers are rounded to nearest 0.5% — they're approximations, but the half-point shift order-of-magnitude matters more than precise calibration. Half-point PIN lines return 0 (push impossible). UFC has no spread/total markets in the conventional sense — push rates are 0.

### Projection math

For TOTAL (line moved by ±0.5):
- Line raised (204 → 204.5): pushes (games totaling exactly 204) now resolve as UNDER. So `p_under += push`, `p_over -= push`.
- Line lowered (204 → 203.5): reverse — pushes now resolve as OVER.

For SPREAD (per-side line moved by ±0.5):
- Lines are stored per side (home -7, away +7 are mirrors). A NEGATIVE delta on a side's own line = harder for that side = prob shifts DOWN. Both home and away use `shift_sign = sign(delta)` — no mirror flip needed (delta itself is the side-specific signal).

For ML: no line, no projection — PIN fair passes through unchanged.

### Failure modes

All silent — never breaks the dossier. Cascading fallbacks:
- PMM SDK call fails → no `polymarket` block on any market → UI shows PIN data only (current behavior pre-PMM).
- PMM event matches but no spread/total market → only ML gets PMM data → others fall back.
- PMM line >0.5 pts from PIN → projection returns None → suggestion falls back to PIN line + PIN fair (consistent pair, never "PMM line at PIN fair" inconsistent).
- Each PMM-related field on the suggestion candidate is null-safe; the frontend renders nothing when fields are missing.

`dossier.pmm_meta` carries `{matched, event_slug, event_title, error}` for diagnostic surfaces.

### Caches

- Event search results: 5 min per `(sport, normalized away/home, ET date)` key. **The date MUST be the US/Eastern game date (PMM's slug convention), NOT UTC — LANDMINE (July 24 2026, the SEA@TEX YRFI no-chip bug):** a 7:05pm Central start is 00:05 UTC the next day, so with a UTC-date key tonight's game and TOMORROW's game of the same series shared one key; the `_pmm_autolog` slug-index loop looks both market rows up seconds apart, the second cache-hit the first game's event (bypassing `_ev_date_ok`, which runs only inside the search), the YRFI slug indexed under both market ids, and the autolog's uniq check skipped it as ambiguous — the resting Poly order never became a pick. Only SUCCESSFUL matches are cached (caching a miss would freeze "no match" for the TTL and kill iteration when tuning matching). Module-level dict in `pmm_markets.py` — survives across requests on a warm Vercel container, cold start resets. **POSTPONED-EVENT LANDMINE (July 29 2026, the Braves@Mets DH zombie):** a postponed event KEEPS its original-date slug while Polymarket moves its startTime onto the makeup day — `_ev_date_ok` therefore treats a slug-date MISMATCH as fatal UNLESS the event's moved startTime agrees with our game within ±3h (that row IS the makeup; a same-day series-mate 6h away stays rejected), and the autolog's slug-index applies the same veto per row. The autolog also removes a FILLED pick that's pre-game with no order + no position (= SOLD; past-start stays for the resolver — settled positions vanish on resolution).
- No BBO cache — quotes are read inline from the markets.list response (embedded `bestBidQuote`/`bestAskQuote`), so there's nothing separate to cache.

### PMM API schema gotchas

The `polymarket_us` SDK's TypedDict declarations for Market/Event don't match the actual API response shape. Things learned the hard way (May 2026 build):

- **Market field is `question`, NOT `title`.** TypedDict declares `title: str` but it comes back null. The human-readable text is in `question`.
- **No `team` field on markets.** Team identity is encoded in `question` text only (e.g., "Spread: Baltimore Orioles (+4.5)" — the team named first is the YES side).
- **Each PMM market is a single binary YES/NO.** Not "one market per side." For a game, PMM has e.g. `Spread: Orioles (+4.5)` as ONE market where YES = Orioles cover. The other side (Rays -4.5) is derived by inverting bid/ask. `pmm_markets._inverse_quote` + `_inverse_side` synthesize this.
- **`outcomes` and `outcomePrices` arrays are null** in markets.list responses. Don't rely on them. Use `bestBidQuote` / `bestAskQuote` (embedded objects with `{value, currency}`) instead.
- **`events.list` returns event metadata with EMPTY `markets` array.** To get the actual markets, follow with `markets.list({eventSlug: [matched_slug]})`. `pmm_markets._search_event` does this; don't remove it.
- **Use `sportsMarketTypeV2` for classification.** `SPORTS_MARKET_TYPE_MONEYLINE` / `_SPREAD` / `_TOTAL`. The lowercase `sportsMarketType` v1 field disambiguates prop variants (skip anything that's not `moneyline` / `spreads` / `totals` / `h2h` — variants like `baseball_team_first_five_total` map to `_TOTAL` but aren't main lines).
- **Line is in `line` field directly.** Floating-point, always positive for spreads (sign is implicit by team perspective). `pmm_push_rates.project_fair_to_half_point` handles the sign flip when projecting to the inverse side.
- **PMM convention for totals: YES = OVER.** If a market's `question` explicitly says "under" we honor it; otherwise default to over.
- **PMM retitled near-term game events to TRICODES July 13 2026 ("NYM vs PHI", slug `mlb-nym-phi-2026-07-16`) → name-only event matching grabbed the WRONG GAME of a series.** The July 13 PMM schema deploy left far-out series games on full-name titles while near/today games went abbreviated; `_match_event_to_game` (full name → mascot → city) couldn't match today's title, fell through to the SATURDAY event of the same series, and returned its sparse markets — symptom triad: dossier "no exchange 1st-inning market" while polymarket.com clearly lists it, pm_snapshots PMM spread/total rows collapsing (far-out events only post ML + a spread or two), and — worst — **the wrong game's PMM prices logged as today's**. Fix (live): Pass 0 matches slug/title tricodes (`_team_code_cands` — Kalshi map via late app import + initial/prefix heuristics) with an **exact ET slug-date match**, and EVERY name pass is date-guarded (`_ev_date_ok`: slug date, else startTime ±14h; no extractable date passes). `_team_mention_pos` also takes ≥3-char tricode tokens so side detection survives if questions go abbreviated too. Diagnosed via `/debug-pmm?sport=MLB&q=<team>` — `diag.matched_slug` carrying the WRONG DATE is the fingerprint.

### What to update when PMM coverage changes

If PMM adds/drops a sport: update `_SPORT_TAG_SLUG` in `pmm_markets.py`. If PMM's market `question` / `sportsMarketTypeV2` format changes, fix `_classify_market`. The `dossier.pmm_meta.error` + matched/event_slug fields make this visible to the UI for debugging.

## Domain Knowledge — Movement

- **Movement / Historical Line Data**: The per-game footer movement bar + the inline sparklines + the click-through chart. All driven by Supabase `book_snapshots`.
  - **Primary opener source**: scanner-backed openers from `/api/openers/scanner` — earliest PIN snapshot per (market, side). PIN-only post-Owls.
  - **Fallback**: legacy Firestore openers in `openers/openers:{sport}` for games predating the cron's history.

### Movement Rules
- **Sharp source**: Pinnacle only. Circa was the historical fallback when PIN dropped lines, but Circa isn't in The Odds API at any region.
- **Opener lock-in**: Once captured for a game ID, PERMANENTLY locked. Never overridden, never reset daily.

### Key Terminology
- **ML** = Moneyline (NOT Machine Learning)
- **SPR** = Spread
- **TOT** = Total (Over/Under)
- **PIN** = Pinnacle (sharp), **DK** = DraftKings, **FD** = FanDuel, **MGM** = BetMGM, **CAE** = Caesars, **HR** = HardRock, **BR** = BetRivers, **BOL** = BetOnline, **LV** = LowVig, **BVD** = Bovada, **ESPN** = ESPN BET, **FAN** = Fanatics, **MB** = MyBookie, **BET365** = Bet365 (US)

## The Odds API (`https://api.the-odds-api.com/v4`)

**Auth**: `?api_key=...` query param (NOT a Bearer header — common gotcha when copying patterns from Owls/etc.)
**Plan**: $59/mo, 100K credits/mo, "All sports / All bookmakers / All markets"
**Cost formula**: each call to `/odds` costs `markets × regions` credits. We send `markets=h2h,spreads,totals` (3) and `regions=us,eu` (2) → **6 credits per call**. With 7 sports × 2 calls/hr × 24h × 30d = 60,480 credits/mo, fits in the 100K budget.

> **Region gotcha**: Pinnacle is in the `eu` region, NOT `us`. Without `eu` in the regions param we'd get zero PIN data — defeating the whole sharp-tracking purpose. The second region doubles the per-call credit cost (6 vs 3), which is part of why the cron uses an adaptive per-sport gate (see "Odds-ingest cron" above) instead of always-on polling.

### Endpoint Used

`GET /v4/sports/{sport_key}/odds?regions=us,eu&markets=h2h,spreads,totals&oddsFormat=american&dateFormat=iso&api_key=KEY`

Response: a top-level JSON array of events, each with a `bookmakers` list, each with a `markets` list (`h2h`/`spreads`/`totals`), each with an `outcomes` list. See `kahla-scanner/scrapers/odds_api.py` for the parse logic.

### Sport Keys
| Scanner code | Odds API sport_key |
|---|---|
| MLB   | `baseball_mlb` |
| NBA   | `basketball_nba` |
| NHL   | `icehockey_nhl` |
| NFL   | `americanfootball_nfl` |
| CBB   | `basketball_ncaab` |
| NCAAF | `americanfootball_ncaaf` |
| UFC   | `mma_mixed_martial_arts` |

### Books Allowlist
The cron + Flask both filter to a 14-book allowlist. Anything else returned by The Odds API (Euro books from EU region — `winamax_fr`, `tipico_de`, `betsson`, `unibet_se`, `marathonbet`, etc.) is silently dropped at ingest. Allowlist must stay in sync between three places:

| File | Symbol |
|---|---|
| `kahla-scanner/scrapers/odds_api.py` | `BOOK_CODES` (Odds API key → short code) + `ALLOWED_BOOKS` (set of allowed short codes) |
| `app.py` | `_SHORT_TO_DISPLAY_KEY` (short code → frontend display key) + `_ALLOWED_BOOKS` (same set) |
| `templates/odds.html` | `BL` + `BL_FULL` + `ALL_KNOWN_BOOKS` |

Allowed short codes (14): `PIN, DK, FD, MGM, CAE, HR, BET365, BR, BOL, LV, BVD, ESPN, FAN, MB`.

### Rate-Limit Headers
- `x-requests-used` / `x-requests-remaining` — logged on every cron run so credit burn is visible in workflow logs.

## Sharp Score (per-market 1-10)

Per-market signal-strength rating shown on each game card's movement bar. Scale of 1-10 where 10 = aggressive sharp signal.

**The unified rule across ML / SPR / TOT:** _sharp side = the side whose bet got HARDER to win._ Books move odds to balance action — whichever side they made worse is where money is flowing. Two distinct sharp signals: a **line move**, OR a **vig-only move** (line flat). Vig drift that comes WITH a line shift is rebalance, NOT a separate signal.

Score is the PIN movement magnitude, full stop. Splits divergence and PIN-vs-retail divergence are NOT folded into the headline number — they're already visible on the card (splits row, per-book odds table) and blending them just dilutes the score when public action happens to be balanced.

Computed JS-side in `computeSharpScore()` (`templates/odds.html`) and Python-side in `_sharp_for_ml/_sharp_for_spread/_sharp_for_total` + `_move_score_ml/_move_score_spr_tot` (`kahla-scanner/scripts/sharp_alerts.py`). Both implementations follow the same rule so the on-card chip and the Telegram alert always agree.

### Score (magnitude)

- **ML**: `|cent_distance(opener, current)|` capped 10. `_amerToCents()` handles the ±100 boundary so a flip from −110 to +110 reads as a 20-cent move, not 0. 1 cent = 1 score, "1 is 1, 5 is 5, 10 is 10".
- **SPR / TOT**: TWO distinct signals, **never additive**.
  - LINE moved (≥0.5pt) → score = `|point_diff| × 10` capped 10. Any juice drift that came along is rebalance, IGNORED.
  - LINE flat → score = `|price_diff_cents|` capped 10. Pure juice move.

### Side detection (which side is sharp?)

| Market | Rule |
|---|---|
| ML | Team whose American odds got more negative (= more expensive to bet = harder = sharp). |
| SPR | PRIMARY: side whose line moved against them (`point_diff < 0` → harder spread to cover). FALLBACK: line flat → side whose price decreased. |
| TOT | Line raised → over needs more = sharp OVER. Line lowered → under has less room = sharp UNDER. Line flat → vig direction. |

Chip prints `[SIDE] SHARP N`. Side label is the team's `truncTeam()` abbreviation for ML/SPR, "OVER"/"UNDER" for TOT.

**Edge case — only one side observed:** if PIN snapshot exists for only one side of a market, we use the available side's direction directly: if it got more favored (negative diff) we fire with that side; if it got less favored we'd be naming the wrong team and don't have the right team's prices to print, so we **skip** the alert/chip rather than label the wrong side. (Old behaviour was an `Infinity` fallback that always picked the available side regardless of direction — that bug is gone.)

The `_splitsSubScore` and `_divergenceSubScore` helpers are kept in the file (Phase 4 Sharp Bot will use them for paper-bet selection logic, where weighted blending across signals makes sense). They just don't feed the on-card display number.

### UI tiers (CSS color-coded chips)

- **0-3** — `tier-low` (gray, muted)
- **4-6** — `tier-mid` (orange)
- **7-9** — `tier-strong` (green)
- **10**  — `tier-elite` (gold gradient)

### Telegram alerts (RETIRED May 2026)

The "sharp alerts bot" Telegram pings (`🚨 STEAM` / `⚡ SHARP N`) were noise — turned off, the cron step removed from `scanner-poll.yml`, `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`/`TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID` now unused. `kahla-scanner/scripts/sharp_alerts.py` survives only as the steam paper-bet logger (runs `STEAM_SILENT=1` — see Sharp Bot below); its Telegram path is a no-op. Full alert logic + the `sharp_alerts` table DDL are in git history if ever resurrected. (The Filled Bot's fill-notification role was retired July 2026 with the Kalshi cutover — its Telegram sender survives for the unlogged-BET alert only.)

**HARD-CUT EARLY — June 19, 2026 (user call: "no reason to wait for the dead date").** The subscription was paid through June 25 but the Pick Bot no longer depends on PIN, so the ingest was retired early. `book_snapshots` is now FROZEN (stops accruing); every PIN reader runs its exchange path or a warm fallback. This section is the working plan; **delete it (replace with a 2-line stub) once the /odds Board rebuild lands — that's the only Pick-Bot-relevant piece left.** User decisions: the Odds Board was RETIRED (not rebuilt) June 2026 — see the RETIRED stub; exchange data is the sole odds source; Kalshi mid is the independent fair anchor for pricing PMM entries.

**Done (June 19 — the Pick Bot brain is fully on exchanges):**
- ✅ Phase 0 — `pm_snapshots` carries PMM spreads/totals per line; Kalshi ML-only. ESPN schedule spine LIVE (`ingest_espn_markets`, odds_api was find-only).
- ✅ Step 1 (sharp score) — **exchange score is now PRIMARY** (was shadow). `_suggest_picks` reads `x_side`/`x_score`; the PIN recency-weighted score is retired with its feed. Bias-corrected: `_X_PP_SCALE` 4.0→5.2 (exchange ran ~0.92 cold vs PIN over 87 paired rows). **PMM+Kalshi confirmation gate (ML-only):** an ML move Kalshi doesn't confirm DEMOTES to a 1u lean (`_xsharp_ml` returns a `confirmed` flag; `unconfirmed_ml` on the candidate). Backed by live data — on PIN/exchange disagreements the unconfirmed side went 3-9.
- ✅ Step 1 (CLV) — `bot_picks_resolver._compute_clv` grades vs the **exchange close** (`_exch_close_pair`: Kalshi mid for ML, PMM for SPR/TOT, devigged; maps `moneyline`→`ml`), PIN `book_snapshots` close only as a warm fallback.
- ✅ Step 2 (fair anchor) — `handicapper_web._attach_exch_current` builds `exch_current` (Kalshi mid / PMM fallback, devigged) per market; `_suggest_picks` reads it as the Polymarket-target + edge base (pin_current is fallback-only).
- ✅ Step 5 (partial) — odds_api ingest step REMOVED from `scanner-poll.yml`. Its adaptive-cadence/`_should_fire`/`odds_pull_requests` machinery is now dead code (git-preserved). `ODDS_API_KEY` can be deleted from env/secrets.
- ✅ BONUS — **prime window is now DATA-DRIVEN + MULTI-ZONE** (weekly auto-tuner, see "Pick Bot — handicapper" + Key Files). The hand-set single (60,180) span was the weak link: the edge is BIMODAL — across 222 settled timed picks the winning bands are **30-90 (+0.21/u) and 150-210 (+0.19/u)** while **90-120 is a losing HOLE (-0.32/u)** and the tails are cold. A single span can't carve the hole, so the prime window is now a **zone list — live `[[30,90],[150,210]]`** (`pickbot_tuning.zones`). `_timing_window` returns `gap` for the 90-120 hole (capped 1u, still bet if the gate clears). ⚠️ **The old "90-120 = +27u proven hammer/core" claim is FALSE on current data — it's now the worst band. `_is_prime_core` (90-120) still tags that band but it's the HOLE, kept only for review labeling.**

**Remaining (owner: any session — work top-down):**
0. **(FOUNDATIONAL — blocks the games list + board once `book_snapshots` stops) Schedule/event spine → ESPN.** Today the `markets` table — the schedule EVERYTHING joins on (`/api/handicapper/games`, pm-snapshot's per-game loop, the dossier, `market_id` on every `bot_picks` row) — is written ONLY by `kahla-scanner/scrapers/odds_api.py`. When odds_api dies nothing populates it, so the games list + pm-snapshot starve. **Decision (June 2026, user): ESPN is the event/schedule/grading spine wherever ESPN covers the sport; the exchanges supply ODDS matched onto ESPN events, NOT the other way around.** ESPN covers everything we run via `site.api.espn.com/apis/site/v2/sports/{grp}/{league}/scoreboard`: the 6 core sports already in `app.py:_ESPN_PATH`, PLUS **MMA (`mma/ufc`, already used by `bot_picks_resolver._ESPN_PATH` to grade UFC ML).** Build a small ESPN→`markets` ingest (one active row per upcoming ESPN game; KEEP the existing UUID `market_id` so `bot_picks`/dossier joins are untouched) to replace odds_api's market-creation role; pm-snapshot + dossier then attach PMM+Kalshi quotes by the team-name + start-time match they already do. **Venue-neutral upside:** a game shows even before an exchange lists its market (vs. PMM-as-spine, where the slate is hostage to the venue's listing time). **The ONE exception (user): MMA method-of-victory props** — `mma/ufc` grades the winner/ML only, so SPR/TOT method bets stay manual-settle, exactly as today. (Soccer/World Cup was on this spine too until the July 2026 soccer kill — spec in git history.) **The general ingest is LIVE (cut over June 2026):** `kahla-scanner/scripts/ingest_espn_markets.py` runs as a step in `scanner-poll.yml` (`--commit`) BEFORE odds_api each tick, and **odds_api is find-only** — `_find_or_create_market` returns None instead of creating when `ESPN_MARKETS_SPINE=1` (set on the odds_api step). So **ESPN is the sole market creator**; odds_api only attaches snapshots to existing ESPN rows. This killed two things at once: duplicate markets, AND The Odds API's broad `mma_mixed_martial_arts` bucket minting GLORY-kickboxing rows under "UFC" (ESPN's `mma/ufc` is UFC-scoped). The `espn-markets-ingest.yml` workflow stays for manual dry-runs / prune / backfills. **Remaining cutover bits still pending below** (CLV, fair anchor, board) are about ODDS sources, not the schedule — the schedule spine is done. ⚠️ **SUPERSEDED FOR MLB (Aug 10 2026) — POLYMARKET IS THE SCHEDULE.** ESPN began 403'ing the Actions runners on the multi-day `dates=A-B` range ~Aug 4; the step is `continue-on-error` + `log.warning`, so it painted a green check every minute for SIX DAYS while creating nothing — `markets` ran dry after Aug 11, the board emptied, and the opener lane reported the healthy gate `all_done` over a pool with nothing in it. `app.py:_pmm_ensure_markets` now mints a `markets` row for every MLB game **Polymarket itself lists** (`_pmm_listed_mlb_keys` → slug tricodes + ET date + the venue's own `startTime`; `_MLB_CODE_TEAM` maps PMM's dialect to our 30 canonical `event_name` strings; `notes.src='pmm_schedule'`), running at the top of `_opener_pass` so a game the venue posts this minute is bettable this minute. **THE LESSON, which the user had stated repeatedly before it was coded: we can only bet what Polymarket lists, so Polymarket's list IS the schedule — a third-party spine can only subtract.** The tick was ALREADY fetching that list every minute and using it merely as a SORT KEY over the ESPN-fed pool. ## Dashboard lane health — `off` is not `broken` (Aug 19 2026)

`_cellar_health` reports a lane switched off at the source as state **`off`**
(muted dot, the word "off" instead of a heartbeat age, its own count beside
the cellar/vercel split) instead of letting it read `stale` and trip
"N need a look" forever. Killing the harvest is what exposed it: a lane we
turned off on purpose was reported as a fault, and `ou_trader` had been in the
same state unnoticed. **A permanent red light on a healthy machine is the same
failure as a silent one** — it trains you to skip the panel, which is exactly
what the card exists to prevent (it was built after the ledger held a
five-second heartbeat for 22 hours while stamping nothing). `_lane_disabled()`
reads the LIVE engine flag rather than a hand-kept list, so flipping an engine
back on restores its monitoring in the same commit — no stale exception list
suppressing a real outage later.

## THE CELLAR CUTOVER — next job (user, Aug 19 2026: "we need it now")

> ⚠ **THE GOAL IS `$0` AND EVERYTHING ON THE BOX — storage, IO, compute, bets,
> model.** Read `docs/cellar-migration-spec.md` **§12** before reasoning about
> any of this; it is the end state and this briefing used to omit it, which is
> exactly how a session came to argue the opposite (below). Vercel Pro $20/mo
> and Supabase Pro ~$25/mo both die. Order: Vercel after Phase 4, **Postgres
> LAST** (§12c) — after the cellar is sole writer and has been boring for 30
> days, and not before a `pg_dump` restore has actually been TESTED (§12e:
> repatriation is done when a restore works, not when Postgres runs locally).
>
> **Two corrections a session got wrong on Aug 20, both already answered in
> the spec:**
> - *"The database should not follow the compute into the basement — the lease
>   needs a shared DB."* **§12c says that is correct for the MIGRATION and wrong
>   as a permanent statement.** Every reason to keep Postgres in the cloud (the
>   seam that lets both sides be live, meaningful lease failover, rollback =
>   stop the daemon) **expires** once the cellar is sole writer. Local Postgres
>   over a unix socket also kills an invisible throttle: every DB call today is
>   a cloud round trip through PostgREST, which starts to matter at a 15s peg.
> - *"Supabase is fine, nothing to do."* **§12d already measured this** (Aug 16:
>   1,738 MB; Aug 20: 1,822 MB — steady state confirmed, retention working).
>   Free tier is 500 MB and is NOT reachable without cutting prop retention to
>   ~4 days, which destroys the bid/ask tape the prop reviews run on. **The $0
>   path is SELF-HOSTING, NOT DOWNGRADING.** 1.8 GB is nothing for a local SSD.
>
> ⚠ **`paperlog` IS ON THE CRITICAL PATH TO `$0`, not an optimization.** While
> it stays on Vercel, Vercel keeps running per-minute compute, so Vercel Pro
> cannot drop to Hobby (§12b). Its blocker is real (see below) but it has to be
> solved, not skipped.
>
> ⚠ **The lane order is DELIBERATE and we deviated from it.** §6 Phase 3 runs
> money lanes LAST (opener is step 6) and holds them until the box is dedicated
> ~mid-Sept, because until then the laptop can be closed by someone not
> thinking about the cellar. **The opener moved FIRST on Aug 20 anyway**, by
> explicit user call ("Football is coming... if I lose a couple of baseball
> bets, OH WELL"). That is an accepted risk, not an oversight, and it degrades
> gracefully: a closed laptop lets the lease go stale and Vercel reclaims the
> lane within its 180s TTL — back to 14s passes, never a blackout.

Vercel's function budget is now the binding constraint on the machine's
thinking. The opener can't walk a slate inside it — a live tick showed
`opener_pool: 4`, four games evaluated out of fifteen — and rule #1's
per-market confirmation added a venue call per slug on top of the dossier
build. On the house box there is no function timeout: the whole slate every
tick, per-market rent checks effectively free.

**Split today (opener moved Aug 20 2026).** cellar: `batch`, `pm_snapshot`,
`ledger`, `vsin`, `kalshi_autolog`, **`opener`**. vercel: `repeg`, `paperlog`,
`alerts`.

✅ **THE MOVE IS MEASURED, NOT ASSUMED.** First cellar opener tick ran
**120,493 ms** — the full self-imposed deadline — against the ~14s of a shared
request it got on Vercel. Same speed per game; roughly 8× the games walked per
pass. Cadence trades down with it: a 120s pass on a 60s schedule skips every
other tick, so the slate sweeps every ~2 min instead of every 1. Far more games
evaluated per hour, which is the number that matters. And Vercel's paperlog
came back to life the same minute — 15/15 requests 200, zero cancellations,
every tick logging `opener={'gate': 'cellar_owns_lane'}`.

⚠ **`paperlog` CANNOT JOIN IT WITHOUT A REFACTOR — and no longer needs to.**
`lanes.CONFLICTS["paperlog"] = {opener, repeg, harvest, ledger, alerts}` and
`Runner.validate` refuses the boot, because the paperlog ROUTE BODY runs those
engines inline. Cross-side that is safe (the lease arbitrates — which is
exactly what `cellar_owns_lane` above shows); in ONE process it is a
double-fire. So moving paperlog means either re-coupling the opener to it
(undoing this) or extracting the engines out of the route (spec §8 item 6).
The cancellation that justified the move urgently is gone — but the move
itself is NOT optional: paperlog on Vercel is what keeps Vercel Pro alive
(§12b). **The extraction is the real work item**, and it is on the path to
`$0`, not a nice-to-have.

✅ **STEP 1 DONE (Aug 20 2026): the lease is ENFORCED.** `CELLAR_LEASE_ENFORCED`
is set on Vercel, so a side that loses a claim actually stands down. Before
this, `_cellar_owns()` claimed, recorded ownership, heartbeated — and returned
`True` REGARDLESS (shadow mode by design), which meant moving a write lane
would have had **both sides placing orders**: duplicate buys, and the
one-order-per-slug invariant the re-peg depends on gone with it (the Aug 16
duplicate incident, on the buy side with real stakes). Arbitration is
selftested 8/8 (renew-own / dead-holder / cellar-preempts-vercel), and
`cellar_claim` bootstraps a missing lane row with a stale heartbeat so a
first-ever claim can't deadlock.

**Step 2 is one line of `.env` on the house box** — add `opener` to
`CELLAR_LANES`, restart the daemon — and two boot guards now stand behind it,
because both failure modes are silent and look healthy:
- **`needs_owner`** — `_autobet_execute` resolves `_kalshi_owner_uid()` and
  returns `False` on `None`, so an opener lane with no admin uid keeps
  persisting its shadow rows (`work>0`, reads ALIVE) and places **zero bets**.
  Worse than the ledger's silent zero, because the tick count lies. The lane
  is flagged, so the runner refuses to start instead.
- **`dry_run_blackout()`** — a money lane claims its lease BEFORE it checks
  `dry_run` (Invariant 1), so `CELLAR_DRY_RUN=1` + `opener` = holds the lease,
  stands the enforced Vercel side down, places nothing. Total betting blackout,
  green on every dashboard. Enabled money lane + dry-run now refuses the boot.
  Rehearse with read-only lanes; there is no rehearsal worth a blackout.

⚠ **A PUSH TO `main` NO LONGER REACHES THE OPENER.** The cellar runs `app.py`
out of a working directory on the house box, so any change to the opener, the
autobet, the re-peg or the gridiron shadows is **inert until someone pulls and
restarts the daemon** — silent, and indistinguishable from a fix that did not
work. Before the cutover, Vercel redeployed every push within a minute. The
daemon now stamps its git SHA at boot (`exec_probe_runs`, `kind=cellar_boot`)
and the dashboard's lane card prints `code <sha>`, or `⚠ cellar on X, site on
Y — pull + restart` when they differ. **Check that line before debugging why a
change "didn't work".**

⚠ **`CELLAR_DRY_RUN` defaults to TRUE, and existing healthy `work=12` ticks are
NOT evidence it is off** — `_drive_route` lanes (pm_snapshot/paperlog/vsin/
kalshi_autolog) ignore the flag entirely; only the direct-call money lanes
check it. Read the boot banner (`mode=`), not the tick counts.

Spec: `docs/cellar-migration-spec.md` §5. Lease table
`kahla-scanner/supabase/cellar.sql`; `CELLAR_SIDE=cellar` on the house box so
it claims as itself (getting that wrong makes the cellar fail its own claim
once enforcement is on, and silently stop running the lane it was moved there
to run). NOT YET GATED and still riding the paperlog route body: `_tg_flush`,
`_bet_alerts`, `_opener_watchdog`.

## TWO LANES: the model bets, and the market maker (direction, Aug 19 2026)

The user's split, stated after the rent rework: **the ACTUAL bets come from
the model on normal lines. The market-maker lane is a separate business on
junk lines.** Rule #1 (rent) gates both; the MODEL gate applies only to the
betting lane, because on a junk line we are deliberately taking no view.

**Model lane (unchanged in kind, shrunk in scope).** Model → rent → bet, in
that order. MLB moneyline is now a **T-6h day-of business** (early was
pulled); NRFI and props were already day-of. **Football spreads are the only
lane where the model and the rent both work across the whole window** — NFL
spread/total pay early + day_of + live, while NFL ML is live-only. And
"football is spreads" is the right expression of the model, not a
consolation: **Gridiron IQ's native output is a projected MARGIN** (off/def
cross + fitted HFA); the win probability is the DERIVED number squeezed
through a logistic. What is missing to bet football spreads is narrow — a
margin distribution to turn projected margin into a cover probability at a
line — and it is fittable from the 1,244 football finals already in
`game_results` (NFL 286, NCAAF 958). Do NOT describe this as "we have no
football spread model"; we have a margin model described by its least useful
projection.

**Market-maker lane (new, unbuilt).** Quote wide "shit lines" nobody else is
playing in — big gaps, early-rent programs — be the touch, and trade back and
forth. Bid at best-bid+1; if it fills, offer at the other touch; if that
doesn't fill, **repeg down toward entry and STOP at entry** (past that you
are paying to be the market maker); if it never sells you held a fair
position and collected rent the whole time. **The spread is the business, not
the rent**: one completed round trip on 10 contracts at 8¢→16¢ is **$0.80**,
against ~$0.02 of rent on the same market — 40×. Rent is what makes waiting
free.

Most of the machinery exists: the re-peg engine already chases a touch,
verifies and re-places; rule #1 confirms per-market that a line pays; the
harvest left post-only sell placement with GTD handling; dedup enforces one
order per slug; rent is attributed per slug so the lane scores itself; and
every sweep gates on `AUTOMATIC`, so hand-placed quotes are never touched.
What's new is quoting BOTH sides, the floor rule at entry, and a
market-selection rule.

**The two open questions, in order:**
1. **Which side to quote.** This venue has shown favorite-longshot bias three
   separate times (K props ≥6/7 YES overpriced 8-9pp; batter TB YES overbought
   3.5-5pp). If that lives in spreads too, the touch on a cheap longshot is a
   few points rich and the money side is the ASK, not the bid — same engine,
   same rent, opposite inventory. Checkable against `pm_snapshots` spread
   history + `game_results`.
2. **Round-trip completion rate.** Not the fill rate on the buy — the fraction
   of buys that exit at a profit. Everything else is bounded and knowable.
   Wide means illiquid on BOTH sides: your buy fills because someone crossed
   down, and nothing guarantees anyone crosses up. First live trial is a
   hand-placed 8¢ bid on `asc-nfl-atl-ind-2026-08-22` (Aug 19).

⚠ **The reward math that reframes sizing.** `target_size` is 15,000 and we
rest 10 contracts, so we collect ~0.07% of a pool. NFL spread early: 10/15,000
× $150 = $0.10; MLB ML early was 10/20,000 × $225 = $0.11; **observed on tight
books: $0.090/market.** Two independent programs matching the observation
means the binding constraint is SIZE against target, not which lines you pick.
Wide books do earn (33 of 176 markets, no hard band) but ~5× less per market —
though that measurement is confounded, since book width is sampled at
PLACEMENT while rent accrues over the whole period and the book may tighten.

## Prediction-market cross-confirm + Pick Bot paperlog (June 2026)

Two new systems built June 2026, both cron-pinged Flask endpoints (curl steps in `scanner-poll.yml`'s `fill_ping` job, reusing `FILLS_CRON_SECRET`):

**1. PMM + Kalshi cross-confirm (the free, low-latency steam detector — Steps 1-2 of 3 built).** The thesis (proven on real snapshots): **Polymarket + Kalshi are deep free exchanges whose cent prices move WITH the sharp number — and our paid Odds-API view of Pinnacle is double-delayed** (their refresh + our cadence + a 11pm-7am blackout), so the free direct feeds LEAD our PINN capture (measured ~4-7 min on KC@MIN, and PINN is blind overnight). Plan: a **1¢ move on BOTH PMM AND Kalshi (same direction)** triggers an on-demand paid PINN pull — event-driven instead of blind-timed.
> - **Kalshi reader** (`_fetch_kalshi_markets` in `app.py`): public, **no API key**. Base `https://api.elections.kalshi.com/trade-api/v2`, `GET /markets?series_ticker=KXMLBGAME&status=open`. **LANDMINE:** prices are dollar STRINGS (`yes_bid_dollars:"0.5100"`=51¢), NOT `yes_bid`/`last_price` (those are null) — `_kalshi_cents()` converts. Two rows per event (`-BOS`/`-NYY`); `yes_sub_title`=the side; ticker `KXMLBGAME-26JUN071335BOSNYY-BOS` = date+ET-time+teams+side. `/debug-kalshi?sport=mlb` dumps the raw shape. Read-only signal — **we don't bet Kalshi**.
> - **Logger** (`/api/pm-snapshot` → `pm_snapshots` table, `_pmm_game_quotes`+`_kalshi_ml_index`+`_match_kalshi`): every ~2 min logs exchange mid-cents for our upcoming games (deduped on cent change), matched to our games by mascot→Kalshi-code map + nearest ET date. **PMM logs ALL main markets — ML + every spread/total line PMM offers** (`market_type`+`line` columns, migration `pm_snapshots_migrations/001_spread_total.sql`, applied June 2026) at zero extra network cost (same `lookup()` call); **Kalshi logs ML everywhere mapped PLUS per-line TOTALS/SPREADS where the suffix encoding is verified (`_KALSHI_LINE_SERIES` — MLB today): `_kalshi_line_events`/`_match_kalshi_lines` log the REAL quoted contracts only (Over N-0.5 / the favorite's -(N-0.5)) as `source='kalshi'` total/spread rows — the SPR/TOT cross-confirm + closing dataset. Recorded, not yet consumed by `_xsharp_two_sided` (promote after rows accrue).** This is the **Odds-API-retirement dataset** — user decided June 2026 to kill The Odds API ($59/mo) and rebuild sharp score / board movement / CLV on PMM+Kalshi history. **SUBSCRIPTION CANCELED, valid through June 25 2026 — the cutover MUST complete before then** (after the 25th `book_snapshots` stops getting rows and every PIN-dependent surface goes stale). Cutover step 1 SHIPPED June 12: the **exchange-based sharp score** (`_pm_history`/`_xsharp_ml`/`_xsharp_two_sided`/`_attach_xsharp` in `handicapper_web.py`) runs SIDE-BY-SIDE with the PIN score — every market's `movement` carries `x_side`/`x_score`/`x_n`, every suggestion + paperlog row carries `x_score`/`x_side`/`x_agree` for the agreement review before the primary flips. Design notes: exchange cents are PROB POINTS (`_X_PP_SCALE`=4 maps them onto PIN's 0-10 scale — calibration constant, tune from the side-by-side data); exchanges quote MULTIPLE spread/total lines at once so there's no discrete line-move event — the score reads cents on the **at-the-money line** (latest cents closest to 50); ML has a **Kalshi-disagree veto** (no signal when the feeds point opposite ways — also absorbs the same-city PMM flip). Remaining before June 25: CLV→exchange close, dossier fair anchor→Kalshi/PMM, odds board rebuild on the two exchanges, flip the sharp-score primary, retire the ingest + adaptive-cadence/trigger machinery. Kalshi = 1 bulk call per in-window sport; PMM per-game under a 7s budget (Vercel 10s). **Validation result: 14/15 games agreed within 0-2¢** — premise confirmed. **GOTCHA — same-city PMM flip:** PMM's matcher swaps sides on two-team cities (Angels/Dodgers, Cubs/White Sox, Mets/Yankees) → PMM and Kalshi disagree >15¢. Kalshi's ticker codes are unambiguous, so the Step-3 trigger trusts Kalshi orientation (a flip just makes the AND not fire = safe).
> - **Step 3 (BUILT June 2026 — event-driven, replaces blind far-out polling):** Flask `/api/pm-snapshot` `_xconfirm_detect` compares each game's PMM+Kalshi HOME cents to a per-game baseline (`xconfirm_state`); when BOTH drift ≥1¢ same direction (>60min to tip, not blackout) it upserts a per-sport `odds_pull_requests` row + resets the baseline. The cron (`odds_api._should_fire`) then **relaxed its cadence**: tight blind ≤3h (2-min terminal, 5-min to the 3h prime edge); FAR out (>3h) is **trigger-only** for `_TRIGGER_SPORTS` (={MLB,NBA,NHL}) — pulls only on a fresh pull-request, **per-sport 5-min cap** (≥5min since last ok run); non-trigger sports keep legacy 15/30-min far cadence. Blackout 11pm-7am MT kept, **plus one 3am MT snapshot** per in-season sport (one-shot via the >30min-since-last-run guard). Net: far-out blind polling killed → credit savings, event-driven freshness. **GOTCHAS:** Kalshi orientation is trusted — a same-city PMM side-flip (Angels/Dodgers, Cubs/White Sox, Mets/Yankees) makes the deltas disagree so the AND **safely no-fires** (no false trigger, but that game won't trigger). NBA/NHL team codes are standard tricodes (SAS/NYK, VGK/CAR confirmed; rest best-effort — a wrong code just fails that game's match). The pm-snapshot **watch window is per-sport** (`_PM_WINDOW_H`: MLB **36h** (12h→36h July 2026 — Poly trades next-day MLB the evening before; at 12h the exchange "opener" was really the T-12h price and every earlier move was invisible to the sharp-score baseline), NBA/NHL 96h, **NFL 168h** — weekly sport, sharp money moves early in the week — **NCAAF 72h**, capped because a 60-game Saturday slate would swamp the budgeted PMM rotation) — sparse/weekly sports are watched far out so we catch their early line movement. **Football wiring (July 2026):** NFL is fully cross-confirmed (`KXNFLGAME` + a 32-team `_TEAM_TO_KALSHI` map — ticker + WAS/JAX/LV codes UNVERIFIED from the sandbox, confirm via `/debug-kalshi?sport=nfl` once games list); **NCAAF and UFC are PMM-ONLY** (no team/fighter map — school codes unmappable blind, fighters aren't teams; the snapshot loop skips the Kalshi bulk fetch for map-less sports). **Single-venue ML rule (DATA-DRIVEN tri-state, July 2026 — replaced a briefly-static `KALSHI_CONFIRM_SPORTS` set the same day):** `_xsharp_ml`'s `confirmed` is `True` (Kalshi agrees) / `False` (Kalshi QUOTES the game — kalshi rows exist in pm_snapshots — but stayed silent; the 3-of-9 noise case, demotes to lean) / `None` (Kalshi has NO rows for the game — unlisted sport or matcher miss; structural absence, NO demotion). `_suggest_picks` demotes only on `x_confirmed is False` — the `is False` is load-bearing. So UFC/NCAAF MLs gate on PMM steam alone automatically, and any sport Kalshi starts quoting gets the confirmation gate the moment its rows appear; `signal_blob.x_confirmed` is tri-state (null = single-venue) for the paperlog review. UFC watch window 168h (96h→168h July 2026 — a Saturday card sat invisible until Tuesday during the All-Star break while its lines drifted; UFC is a weekly card sport like NFL, watch the full week); **UFC Kalshi is WIRED and the ticker is CONFIRMED (live probe July 4 2026): `KXUFCFIGHT`** — 24 open markets on the Jul 11 card, title shape `'Will {Fighter} win the X vs Y professional MMA fight scheduled for {date}?'`. Name-matched fighter index (`_kalshi_ufc_index`/`_match_kalshi_ufc` — fighter from `yes_sub_title` OR parsed from the title (the verified shape), resolver-style last-name-token match, no ticker-code map; **token filter is ≥2 chars, NOT ≥3 — LANDMINE (July 2026): 'Seokhyeon Ko' vs Kalshi's 'Seok Hyun Ko' only share the 2-letter token 'Ko', and the old ≥3 filter dropped it, blinding every match layer for that fight — the user caught it live ('no exchange data' on a fight Kalshi had at $5.7k vol). Same rule in `_kmf_ufc`/`_kmf_ufc_ml`; don't re-tighten**); the snapshot loop tries `_KALSHI_UFC_SERIES_CANDIDATES` first-hit-wins. Once UFC kalshi rows appear in pm_snapshots, the tri-state confirm gate applies to UFC automatically (no constant to flip). **Kalshi also runs UFC props** — `KXUFCROUNDS` (round-count O/U), `KXUFCMOV`/`KXUFCMOF` (method), `KXUFCDISTANCE` — future material for the manual-settle prop lane. **`/debug-kalshi-discover`** (v2) is the general new-sport tool: series-list endpoint + `?probe=TICKER,..` direct market probes (`?q=ufc` auto-probes the UFC candidates); then `/debug-kalshi?series=TICKER` for the raw market shape. SPR/TOT gate normally everywhere (totals are dark for non-MLB regardless — `TOTALS_TEST_MODE` is MLB-scoped; UFC 'totals' on PMM are round-count O/U and its 'spreads' are method props — auto-grade stays ML-only, gotcha unchanged). The snapshot loop also has a **near-game priority tier**: games starting ≤6h jump the stale-first rotation queue, so the September MLB+NFL+NCAAF triple slate can't starve in-prime cent freshness. The Pick Bot **games list** is a separate, per-sport display window (2 days most sports / 7 for football — `_display_window_end`; decoupled from watch/pick/trigger). Sport-driven config: `_KALSHI_SERIES` + `_TEAM_TO_KALSHI` + `_PM_SPORTS` (app.py) + `_TRIGGER_SPORTS` (odds_api) — add all four to cover a new sport.

**2. Pick Bot paperlog (`/api/handicapper/paperlog` → `pickbot_paperlog` table).** A **silent, complete log of every GATE-CLEARED suggestion** the bot makes over the **5h→1min** pre-game window (whether or not the user bets it) — the always-on dataset for reviewing which suggestions/timing-windows actually win, separate from the user's real `bot_picks` and the killed `paper_bets`. **No forced leans** (write-time filter), with ONE deliberate exception: **VSiN-VETOED would-be picks shadow-log** as `gates_cleared=false` + `signal_blob.vsin_vetoed_pick=true` (July 2026) — built when the veto was live, now DORMANT: **the Circa veto was demoted to WARN-ONLY hours later** (see the VSiN backtest note in the vsin-snapshot row above), so no picks get vetoed and cleared picks just carry `signal_blob.vsin_veto=true` (the would-have-warned flag) — measured directly on real rows. **Dedup = "log on bet CHANGE" per (game, market_type, VARIANT):** the variant discriminator ('' real / 'model' shadow spread model / 'veto' VSiN-vetoed shadow / 'ufcdur' Fight IQ duration shadow / 'ufcveto' Fight IQ-vetoed ML shadow, derived from signal_blob) is REQUIRED — **LANDMINE (June 28 2026 flood):** without it the steam-spread and shadow-model rows shared the `(market_id,'spread')` key, alternated (side,units) every tick, and logged 239 rows that day, inflating the shadow spread record to a fake 131-13 (real: 16-13, −1.7u). 227 dup rows deleted July 2026 (backup: `pickbot_paperlog_dups_bak`). A row lands each time the suggested **(side, units)** changes; unchanged-bet price drift is NOT a row (that's in `pm_snapshots`). Logs ML/SPR/TOT + NRFI (NRFI at 1u — matches live sizing; the writer was stale at 0.5u until July 2026, so older NRFI paperlog pnl is half-scale); `entry_price`=PMM maker bid (else fair). Every suggestion row records `signal_blob.circa_move_pp` (Circa handle trajectory on the picked side). **Bet-time Pinnacle stamp (July 2026, parlay-api.com free tier):** every REAL gate-cleared main-market row also gets `signal_blob.pin` `{price,line,opp_price,at}` — Pinnacle's contemporaneous quote via `app.py:_pin_stamp_rows`/`_pin_slate` (TOA-compatible API, `bookmakers=pinnacle`, ONE slate pull = ~3 credits, DB-cached 90 min in `parlay_state` so cold starts don't refetch; HARD budget stop at 900 of the 1,000 free credits/mo, usage counter in the same table; key in `parlay_state.api_key` or `PARLAY_API_KEY` env). NOT a polling feed — a sniper for the PIN-agree-vs-disagree review (the methodology that graded the MLB power model out of sizing). DDL `kahla-scanner/supabase/parlay_state.sql` (applied). The weekly tuner is protected: it filters `gates_cleared=true` AND skips `signal_blob.spread_model` rows. **Budgeted ~8s, RANDOM-shuffle rotation** (July 2026 — the old stale-first sort keyed on last row INSERT time, but rows only insert on bet change, so zero-bet games monopolized the budget and fresh-bet games starved; caught when an unlogged-bet alert never fired). Every in-window game gets an equal shot per tick (build_dossier per game is heavy). **Graded by `bot_picks_resolver._resolve_paperlog()`** — isolated pass after the bot_picks heartbeat, reusing the same ESPN-match + to-WIN + CLV helpers; grades ALL pending rows including veto shadows (no gates_cleared filter). **GOTCHAS:** `_grade` reads `entry_line` but the table column is `line` → aliased in the loop; `result_score` is stored as JSON text (the column is `text`, not jsonb like bot_picks); **review queries MUST filter variants** — real picks = `gates_cleared and coalesce(signal_blob->>'spread_model','')<>'true'`, veto counterfactual = `signal_blob->>'vsin_vetoed_pick'='true'`.

## Pick Bot — handicapper (dual flow)

Different from Sharp Bot. Sharp Bot is fully automated (cron picks from rule-based logic). Pick Bot is **interactive**: the user types a game name and gets the full pre-game data + a rule-based pick suggestion + the option to either log it directly or pull a long-form narrative from Claude.

**Two flows, same data**:
- **Web (`/handicapper`)** — Sport tabs at the top, click-to-pick game list below them. Click "Pick" on any game → live dossier renders with: PIN devig fair (Polymarket target), retail prices for reference (NOT recommended bet venues), Action Network splits, ESPN injuries + records + last 10, MLB pitchers, **Team comparison** block (RPG/ERA/OPS/BA for MLB; PPG/PAPG/etc. for ESPN sports — labeled `REFERENCE · NOT IN PICK`, never affects the suggestion), and the bot's rule-based suggestion(s) at the top. Up to 2 picks per game (one of {ML, SPR} + optional TOT). Search bar is collapsible (typing fallback) — click is primary. **Best for browsing a card and grabbing quick picks.**
- **In-chat Claude (mobile or desktop)** — Ask "Toronto vs Angels today, thoughts?" in any Claude Code session in this repo. The `/handicap` skill auto-loads, runs `kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper.py` for the same dossier, and Claude writes the full narrative analysis applying the strategy rules (PIN anchor, line movement read, public splits divergence, fade-the-public, late-scratch caveats). **Best for the deep narrative read.** Hand-off bridge: the web page has a "Copy for Claude" button that copies the dossier JSON formatted for paste into Claude Code.

**Markets**: ML, spread, total, plus **NRFI/YRFI** (first-inning, MLB only — see "NRFI / YRFI" section below). No other props.

**Multi-pick per game.** The dossier's `suggestions` field is a list. The bot may recommend up to 2 picks per game: one of {ML, SPR} plus an optional TOT (TOT currently BLACKLISTED — see the total-side-veto note below). ML and SPR are mutually excluded — they're correlated bets on the same direction, so the bot picks exactly one. The choice is a **symmetric chalk filter** on the ML fair price:
- **ML fair ≤ -140 (chalky)** → drop ML, keep SPR. The leveraged spread is the cleaner expression of a chalky directional bet at +EV prices.
- **ML fair > -140 (lighter)** → drop SPR, keep ML. SPR's variance isn't worth it when ML is reasonable; just bet the cleaner side.

The decision runs on `by_market` BEFORE the gate-clearing step, so SPR can't sneak into the picks just because its `sharp_score` outranks an unsignaled ML on a near-pickem game. Sharp signal still gates whether a pick is "real" (≥4) vs a forced 1u lean — it just doesn't decide which of ML/SPR you bet. Earlier versions picked the better-priced expression (bigger fair_american) outright, then pivoted to higher-sharp_score — both are variance reasoning, not edge reasoning. The legacy `suggestion` field is kept as an alias for `suggestions[0]` for backward-compat.

**Out-of-band SPR is filtered — SYMMETRIC ±150 (INVARIANT, do not half-code).** A SPR candidate is dropped before pick selection whenever its `fair_american` is outside `[-150, +186]` (60¢–35¢, Poly whole cents) — too chalky (`<= SPR_CHALK_FAIR_CAP` -150, a worse-EV leveraged ML) OR too longshot (`>= SPR_LONGSHOT_FAIR_CAP` +186, a leveraged-dog runline with no edge). A spread only shows in the 35¢–60¢ band where the leverage has value. **LANDMINE (June 2026):** only the chalk half (-150) was ever coded, so longshot runlines (e.g. `Reds -1.5 @ +192 fair / +292 entry`) leaked through as forced leans for months — the "+292 should never show" bug. Both halves now live in the `candidates` filter in `_suggest_picks`; keep them together. **Scoped to SPR only** — NOT ML (a +155 ML dog is a legit bet the user takes; a blanket +150 cap would block their own bets) and not TOT (always ~±120). ML at heavy chalk is also not filtered (no correlated alternative; the heavy ML IS the cleanest expression).

**Always-give-a-pick (SERVER-side only — leans are INVISIBLE on the page, July 26 2026).** `_suggest_picks` still always returns at least one candidate when exchange data exists (the paperlog's shadow dataset depends on it — don't "fix" the server to pass), but the UI renders ONLY `gates_cleared=true` suggestions. User call: "Kill leans on the dashboard, just show bets" — the games-list lean chips (briefly back at 0.5u in July, went 5-6 −0.15u), the dossier's orange "Lean — would pass" card, and the lean legend/CSS are all GONE. A dossier whose suggestions are all leans shows a compact "No bet — nothing clears the sharp gate" note; the markets grid below stays loggable. In-chat is unchanged (Claude still says "I'd pass — [why]" + the forced lean).

**Sticky gate (hysteresis, June 2026) — fixes the "pick exists for 5 minutes then vanishes" regression.** The June recency-weight trim made a genuine 5¢ PIN steam clear the gate (3) only while <15min old (5×0.75≈3.75), after which it decayed below 3 (5×0.5=2.5) and the pick demoted back to a lean — picks/day fell from ~15 to ~5 and chips flickered. Fix is a Schmitt trigger, NOT un-trimming the weights: ENTER at `SHARP_SCORE_MIN` (3); once a (market_type, side) has cleared the gate for a game (memory = `pickbot_paperlog` gate-cleared rows via `_sticky_keys`, 12h lookback `STICKY_LOOKBACK_H`), it STAYS a real pick while its score holds ≥ `STICKY_GATE_EXIT` (1.5). The total-side veto still overrides. Sticky picks re-size through Kelly (typically 1u) and carry `sticky=True` → `signal_blob.sticky` so the paperlog review can compare sticky vs fresh-gate picks. **The paperlog row written during the move's FRESH window is exactly the memory the hysteresis reads — don't decouple them.** Lives in `_suggest_picks` (`sticky_keys` param) + `_sticky_keys` in `handicapper_web.py`.

**~~Lean chips~~ — REMOVED June 2026 (user: "stop showing picks outside the prime window").** The games-list line 2 no longer renders forced leans or "outside pick window" notes. It shows ONLY gate-cleared picks that are IN their prime window, plus any pick the user has already logged (green "✓ already picked", shown regardless of window). See the "Prime-only games list" note below.

**Reason bullets on the suggestion card.** Each suggestion renders 2-3 plain-English bullets directly on the card explaining the read (line move, public splits divergence, Polymarket fair). Generated by `_buildReasons()` in `templates/handicapper.html` from the dossier — same function pre-fills the log-pick modal so the bullets the user sees ARE the bullets that get saved on the row. Mirror Python helper `auto_reasons()` in `kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper_log_pick.py` auto-generates the same bullets when a chat-side pick is logged with `--signal-blob` but no `--reason` flags, so chat-logged picks aren't blank on `/handicapper`.

**Public splits in the dossier — single source, shared scraper.** `handicapper_web.py:_fetch_splits` does NOT re-implement Action Network scraping. It late-imports `app.py:_fetch_action_splits(sport)` — the same orchestrator that powers the `/odds` page splits row — then matches the returned events to the dossier's (away, home) by two-way team-name substring containment. Single source of truth: when Action's API or `__NEXT_DATA__` shape shifts, fixing it in `app.py` fixes it for the Pick Bot dossier too. Returns `{away_bets, home_bets, away_money, home_money, sharp_diff, sources, sources_tried, per_source}`. `sources = ["action"]` when matched, `[]` when not.
