(Earlier versions of this code tried to blend in Covers and VegasInsider as redundancy; both were broken without browser-verified selectors, AND Action carries the only money% — the sharp signal — so dropping them removed unused complexity.) PIN line movement is still primary — public data is secondary, weighted 30% in `combined_score` only when the sharp side has movement (70% PIN sharp / 30% splits).

**Splits diagnostic surface.** When `_fetch_splits` returns `sources: []` (no event matched the dossier's away/home), the page renders an orange empty-state row showing per-source state from `per_source.action`: events_returned, sample_games (first 5 from Action's response), and `fetch_debug` (source path used: `table` / `next_data` / `json_api`, plus inner debug from each parser). If you see this row and Action returned games but yours isn't in the sample, that's a team-name match issue. If Action returned 0 events, look at the `next_debug` / `api_debug` to see if the upstream parsers broke (most likely cause: Action moved their JSON envelope or renamed fields). Iterate fixes in `app.py:_parse_action_splits_next_data` / `_next_data_event` — those are shared with `/odds`.

**Per-market grid shows ALL three markets.** ML / SPR / TOT panels all render under the suggestion card regardless of which the bot picked, so the user can log a pick the bot didn't suggest (e.g., bot picked TOT UNDER, user wants ML home). The bot-picked side(s) get a green-tinted background + "BOT PICK" tag on the market header + a blue "Log bot pick" button (uses `openLog(false, idx)` — same flow + seeded reasons/units as the suggestion card). Every other side has a grey "Log this side" button that fires `pickSideFromGrid(mt, side)` — seeds the log modal with PIN devigged fair as the Polymarket target, defaults to 1u/low (no bot signal backing it), and only credits sharp_score if THIS side is the side the weighted PIN movement actually pointed at (else 0). Was previously pick-scoped (hid non-picked market panels); rendering them costs nothing and the green highlight keeps the bot's recommendation clearly distinguished.

**Dossier opens as a modal overlay.** Clicking Pick on a game card opens the dossier in a centered modal (max-width 920px) layered over the games list with a dimmed backdrop, NOT inline at the bottom of the page. Dismiss with the × button, Esc, or backdrop-click. Body scroll is locked while the modal is open so the games list behind it doesn't drift; the modal scrolls internally. Closing returns to the exact games-list scroll position so you can click the next game without re-finding your spot.

**Sport tabs sort dynamically by upcoming-games count.** Tabs at the top of `/handicapper` re-order on page load + every 5 min so sports with the most games sit on the left. NFL / NCAAF drop to the far right in summer; UFC slides leftward when its slate gets posted. Each tab renders a small count badge ("MLB 15", "UFC 4"); zero-game sports show label-only. The active tab auto-scrolls into view if the re-sort pushes it past the visible window. Backed by `/api/handicapper/sport-counts` (one query, all sports).

**Color-tiered kickoff timer.** The `.when` cell on each game row colors by urgency: ≥ 2h → grey (default), 1-2h → yellow, 15-60m → green, < 15m → red bold. Live/done games render as default grey "live/done" string. Pure visual cue — sort order is still by `event_start` ascending. **The countdown text + color tier tick live every 60s** via `_tickKickoffs()` — walks the rendered `.when[data-start]` cells and recomputes label + tier from the stored `event_start`. Pure DOM update, no re-fetch, no API cost; "57m" ages to "56m" and a game crossing into the <15m window turns red on its own. The 5-min `_refreshSportTabOrder` still handles re-sorting + picking up newly-published games.

**Prime-only games list + one-tap log chips (admin/bot_access only).** Every game row is two lines: line 1 = kickoff time + matchup + a neutral **`Details ›` button** (opens the full dossier via `pickGame`); line 2 = the bot's **prime-window recommended bets as one-tap log chips** — or empty. **DECLUTTER REV (June 2026, user: "this screen is awful, just the pick + units"):** line 2 now shows ONLY:
- **Gate-cleared picks IN their prime window** — `_renderPickLines` shows a pick chip only when `p._tw === 'prime'` (the server-resolved per-market `timing_window`, threaded through the verdict cache; NRFI uses the pooled `_isPrime`). No leans, no "outside pick window" / "evaluating…" / "no pick" notes — if nothing qualifies, line 2 is blank.
- **Logged picks — ALWAYS** (green `✓ logged`/`✓ already picked`), regardless of window, so the user never loses sight of a bet. Drawn from the verdict cache when present, else synthesized from `_loggedPicks` via `_loggedChipLabel` (side + units, no price).
- **MODEL bets (NRFI + TOT) use an EARLY window, NOT the steam zones (June 2026).** ML/SPR are steam bets → prime is near tip (their tuned `zones_by_market`). NRFI and model-driven TOT are MODEL bets (matchup vs price) → their edge is EARLY, before the market sharpens. Paperlog proof: NRFI ≥2h30m went **88-81 +8.88u** vs <2h30m **17-20 +0.06u** (dead); model-TOT lives at 3h+. So `_renderPickLines` gates `market_type ∈ {nrfi, total}` on `diffMin >= MODEL_BET_EARLY_MIN` (150 = 2h30m), while ML/SPR gate on `_tw === 'prime'`. This is why NRFI no longer shows at ~2h (that's LATE for a model bet). Provisional hardcode — the real fix is to teach the weekly tuner to carve NRFI/TOT zones from their own paperlog (they're currently EXCLUDED from it).
- **No make/take/book pills.** The `.mt-pill` directive was REMOVED from the cards (it was the clutter). The make/take read still lives on the dossier (Details) only — `_prefetchVerdicts` no longer fetches `_fetchMakeTakeVerdict` for the cards.
- **The PRIME glow + badge are derived from the displayed chips, not a zone-guess (June 2026 — the "everything flashes PRIME on load" fix).** A row glows + shows `Prime · {markets}` IFF `_renderPickLines` actually rendered a **non-logged, in-prime pick chip** on it (the `primeLabels` set built in the same loop). Logged-only rows do NOT glow. `renderGamesList` paints rows prime-NEUTRAL and `_renderPickLines` (called right after, and on every 60s tick + prefetch) sets the glow — so a row never flashes PRIME before its verdict evaluates, and you can't get "PRIME · TOT" with no chip (the old `_rowPrime` used the pooled TOT zone while the chip used the ≥2h30m model window — removed). While a verdict is still loading for an in-window game (no cache yet), line 2 shows a subtle **`evaluating…`** note; once evaluated it's either the chips or blank. The dead `_rowPrime`/`_isPrime`/`_primeBadgeText` zone helpers were retired.

Tapping a chip logs that bet instantly at the recommended units via `oneTapLog()` — TRUE one-tap, no modal (re-reverted July 12 2026 with the Kalshi cutover; the brief modal detour existed only for the dead ProphetX fill field). The tap fetches the live Kalshi make/take verdict first so the recorded entry = the real Kalshi resting bid (else the prefetched exchange price), with an in-flight tap-key guard against double-taps; upgrade taps log the DELTA directly. The dossier's Log buttons keep the modal (reasons/units editing). Chip color = unit tier (`.u1` blue / `.u3` amber; 5u gone) OR state via `_pickState` (green `✓ logged`, violet `↑ upgrade`); `conflict` (opposite/correlated side of a logged bet) is silently skipped — never shown. Driven by `_prefetchVerdicts()` — throttled (2-concurrent) cache-only dossier fetches for games within `VERDICT_WINDOW_MS` (3.5h, covers the outer prime edge), re-run on render + every 5 min + every 60s tick. Caches `{picks:[{market_type, side, units, _chip, _tw, _body}], fetchedAt}` per `market_id` in `_verdictCache` (TTL 5 min); `picks` = every gate-cleared suggestion (NO leans) + the NRFI bet side (1u). `_loggedPicks` (market_id → Map(market_type → {units, side}), PENDING rows only) drives logged/upgrade and the always-show logged chips. **Cost:** zero Odds API credits. **Viewer-safe:** `_renderPickLines`/`_prefetchVerdicts` early-return on `!_canBet`.

**Market groups — never prompt the opposite side of a logged bet.** ML and SPR are the SAME directional bet (the bot picks one or the other, never both), so `_mktGroup()` files them under a shared `'side'` group; TOT and NRFI are their own groups. `_pickState(mid, mt, side, units)` classifies a candidate against `_loggedPicks` into `fresh`/`upgrade`/`logged`/`conflict`/`new`: a candidate whose GROUP is already logged (e.g. you bet Twins ML, bot now likes Pirates -1.5 SPR) is **`conflict`, NOT `new`** — so neither the dashboard button nor the dossier ever nudges you to add the opposite/correlated side of a game you're already on. Both `_renderPickLines` (the line-2 chips) and the suggestion-card renderer key off `_pickState`, so they agree. `_loggedPicks` stores `{units, side}` per market_type and the verdict-cache picks carry `side` for this.

**The dossier speaks the same color language.** When you open a game, the suggestion card's primary button mirrors the dashboard via `_pickState`: violet `.up` ("Add to Nu" upgrade / "Log new pick" new), green `.done` ("✓ Logged"), neutral `.muted` ("Log anyway" + a red `⚠ opposite of your ML` badge for a `conflict`), else unit-tier colored (`.t3` amber / `.t5` gold / `.t05` muted; 1u blue). The market-grid "Log bot pick" button is tier-colored too. NRFI/YRFI card buttons use `.nrfi-actions` styling (the +EV side is a green `.bet` button) to match the rest — they were previously unstyled/garish.

**Logging a pick does NOT close the dossier.** `submitLog` closes only the log modal (`closeLog()`) and shows a transient `_toast('Pick logged ✓')`, keeping the game's dossier open so you can log multiple bets on the same game (e.g. ML + TOT + NRFI) back-to-back without re-opening it. `loadData(false)` refreshes the pending/settled lists + re-colors the games buttons quietly underneath. (Was `closeLog()+closeDossier()` — closing every time was a pain for multi-bet games.)

**PIN open→current is always shown per market, regardless of sharp score.** Each market panel's per-side PIN row appends `(open -183)` / `(open 104 @6.5)` whenever PIN's price or line differs from the opener — independent of whether the recency-weighted sharp score cleared any threshold. Plus a per-card `PIN opened Nh ago` footer. The sharp-score chip (`SHARP {side} = {N} recency-weighted`) is now JUST the "how much does it count" annotation and only renders when a side scored ≥ 1. Rationale: a visible PIN move that scores ~1 because it's old/stale (recency-weighted down) was previously invisible (chip suppressed when score below threshold), making it look like "no movement captured." Now you always see the move + its age, with the score as separate context. Lives in `renderMarketCard` in `templates/handicapper.html`.

**Execution venue = POLYMARKET ONLY (July 24 2026 — user: "back to betting exclusively on Polymarket... push Kalshi back to dormant on everything outside the pick bot model").** One constant gates it: **`app.py:KALSHI_EXECUTION = False`** — flip to `True` and the dual best-execution engine (July 2026 rev) is back, every Kalshi reader is kept wired. Dormant means: the make/take verdict builds ONLY the Poly book (`_pmm_side_book`/`_pmm_side_entry` → `_pmm_book`), so `entry_book` is always `POLYMARKET`; the **Kalshi autolog does not run** (no picks created from / removed by Kalshi holdings — the Polymarket autolog is the sole book-of-record); the **fill tracker skips the 3 authed Kalshi portfolio reads per poll** (this was the dual-venue dashboard slowness the user flagged — a leftover KALSHI-book pending pick shows fill `unknown`, renders no chip, and still grades via the resolver). `_applyMakeTakeToBody` stays verdict-driven (defaults POLYMARKET; with no Kalshi book fed it can only choose Poly). The recorded `entry_price` is the Poly resting-bid make (maker-only default: rest the bid, log the bid — CLV + to-WIN key off it; the sub-cent maker fee/rebate rides outside the logged price). A reader miss → the seeded exchange price stands (log modal flags a READER GAP) — per RULE 0.001 a miss is a coverage bug (`/debug-crossbook`), never a venue fact. A real fill at a different price is corrected via the **Edit modal** or the fill tracker's auto-sync (see the fill-status row). The user does NOT bet retail sportsbooks. Resolver grades against ESPN final scores. **Kalshi stays FULLY ALIVE as the Pick Bot SIGNAL/model — pm_snapshots cross-confirm logger, the `x_confirmed` ML gate, `exch_current` fair anchor, the CLV exchange close, the NRFI price fallback, UFC bout times, `/debug-kalshi*` — none of that is gated.** See "Polymarket Market Lookup" for the PMM data source. VENUE HISTORY (design for whiplash): Kalshi routed Jun–Jul 2026 → de-venued Jul 5 → sole venue Jul 12 → dual best-execution ~Jul 20 → **POLY-ONLY Jul 24 via `KALSHI_EXECUTION=False` (this)**.

**Make-vs-take execution engine — POLY-ONLY since July 24 2026 (`KALSHI_EXECUTION=False`; the Kalshi leg below is wired-but-dormant, exactly mirroring how the Poly leg survived the July 12 "100% Kalshi" era — flip the constant and both venues compare again; ProphetX stays DEAD, all code removed).** The Kalshi reader coverage below is kept accurate for the flip-back. **Backend `GET /api/handicapper/make-take`** (`@bot_required`; params `sport`/`away`/`home`/`side`/`market_type`/`line`/`units`/`starts_in_min`; the old `slug`/`inverse` PMM params are accepted and ignored) builds the Kalshi book via `_kalshi_side_book` (ML) / `_kalshi_line_book` (MLB total+spread), series-disambiguated by `_kalshi_nearest` (event time reconstructed from `starts_in_min`), and returns the cheapest fillable of **{MAKE (rest at bid), MAKE+ (rest at bid+1¢ — Kalshi tick is 1¢ — stepping in front of the queue, still a maker), TAKE (cross at fee-inclusive ask)}**. Reader miss → `available:false` with a reader-gap reason (RULE 0.001: Kalshi lists everything — fix coverage, don't blame Kalshi). **Coverage (July 12 2026 rev):** ML = MLB/NBA/NHL/NFL (`_KALSHI_SERIES` team codes) + UFC (`_kmf_ufc_ml` — fighter-NAME matched against KXUFCFIGHT, opponent-in-title + ±2d date guard; fighters have no code map so the generic ML resolver can never match a fight); totals+spreads = MLB verified + NBA/NHL/NFL candidates (`_KALSHI_LINE_SERIES_X` — MLB-pattern suffix guesses, fail-safe); **NRFI** (`_kalshi_nrfi_book`, series-name candidates + title-sniffed YES orientation); **UFC distance/rounds** (`_kalshi_ufc_prop_book` → KXUFCDISTANCE/KXUFCROUNDS, fighter-name title match). On top of the static candidates every reader falls back to a RUNTIME search of Kalshi's own `/series` catalog (`_kalshi_series_list`, 6h cache → `_kalshi_find_series`), so a series Kalshi lists under an unguessed name is found without a deploy. All misses degrade to no-book (the seeded exchange price logs); unverified suffix schemes can only MISS, never select a wrong line. Verify any candidate via `/debug-kalshi-discover?q=total|spread|inning|ufc` (auto-probes the candidate lists). The verified `_KALSHI_LINE_SERIES` map stays MLB-only because it also feeds the pm_snapshots LOGGER — promote a candidate there only after its suffix encoding is confirmed. Engine mechanics (in `_cross_book_signal`/`add()`):
> - **THE TAKE RULE (`_TAKE_IMB = 1.5`)** — TOP ROW (the touch) ≥1.5× more contracts BID than OFFERED ⇒ a resting maker joins the back of a queue sellers aren't clearing → TAKE. Top row ONLY, **no size floor, no spoof gate** (RULE 0.001 — these books are deep; the old 2-level read + `_TAKE_MIN_QUEUE` floor are gone). Returns `touch_imbalance`/`queue_ahead`/`ask_touch` for the Details read.
> - **Fee-aware.** Kalshi sports: taker `7·p(1−p)`¢/contract (steep — ~1.75¢ at 50/50, rounded UP on the order total), **maker = 25% of taker (`1.75·p(1−p)`¢) — PROVEN July 12 2026 on the user's own executed KXMLBGAME order via `/api/kalshi/probe` (`maker_fees_dollars 0.0115` on 3@32¢ = exactly 3×0.3808¢ rounded up). The same-day "$0 maker" read came off resting-ticket PREVIEWS, which don't show maker fees — trust fills, not previews.** Reported take price is fee-inclusive (`take_eff`). Poly's fee/rebate helpers survive for the dormant leg.
> - **Clock fallback.** Balanced book + `≤30m to tip → TAKE` (a maker won't fill in time) unless the touch is ask-heavy (sellers hit your bid fast → still MAKE). One-tick-spread exception: when spread ≤ tick there's no MAKE+ lane, and an ask-leaning one-tick book means the bid IS the front of the queue → MAKE.
> - **No synthetic-side inversion on Kalshi** — it lists two real rows per game (one per team), so the PMM `_invert_book` landmine class disappears from the live path. (`_invert_book` still serves `_kalshi_line_book`'s under/dog sides — totals/spreads are one YES contract per line — and the dormant PMM leg.)
>
> **Frontend (`handicapper.html`).** The verdict renders on **Details** (`.mt-chip` + `.mt-detail` via `_loadAllMakeTake`, `.mt-wrap[data-mid]` — no PMM slug involved) and as the **log modal's execution line** (`_lmLoadExec` → "MAKE at 46¢ · book 46¢/47¢" + the Pinnacle reference; "No Kalshi market found for this bet." when Kalshi doesn't list it). `_makeTakeCache` (keyed `mid:mt:side`) is populated by both paths, and **logging follows the verdict**: `_applyMakeTakeToBody` (submitLog, all log paths) stamps `entry_book='KALSHI'` unconditionally and overrides `entry_price` with the best raw make when a verdict is cached (the sub-cent maker fee rides outside the logged price; the execution rec is stashed in `signal_blob.execution` for the review). Viewer-safe (`!_canBet` early-returns).

**Click-to-pick is cache-only — no per-click Odds API burn.** The Pick button on the games list used to pass `live=true` (6 credits/click) to get moment-fresh lines, but the new adaptive cron polls every 5 min when nearest game is < 2h out, so cached data is fresh enough. Dossiers now always pull from Supabase. The freshness label "PIN Xm ago · cron Ym ago" tells you how fresh — PIN half = last time PIN's price/line changed (book_snapshots dedup, so unchanged PIN doesn't advance this), cron half = last successful Odds API ingest for this sport from `odds_ingest_runs`. Cron half turns red when > 10m old (broken-cron symptom). Auto-refresh polls the dossier every 30s from cache while the modal is open. The `live=true` query param still works on the backend for manual curl debugging — no UI exposes it.

**Live event matching.** Substring containment for non-UFC sports, with a 30-min `commence_time` window. UFC: 6h window (cards span 5-6h with each fight having a different `commence_time` in The Odds API but a single card-start time in our `markets` table) + name normalization (collapse non-alphanumeric → handles `Cortes-Acosta` vs `cortes acosta` and diacritics) + last-name token fallback (`B. Susurkaev` matches `Baysangur Susurkaev`) + home/away orientation swap (UFC home/away assignment is arbitrary; if standard orientation fails, the matcher tries the swap and shallow-copies the event with `home_team`/`away_team` flipped so downstream side-routing is correct).

**Resolution / grading.** `bot_picks_resolver` runs every cron-job.org tick (1 min — was 30 min before May 2026) as the last step of `scanner-poll.yml` (`continue-on-error: true` on the workflow step). Was originally `RESOLVE_LAG_HOURS=4`; dropped to **0** — ESPN's `state='post'` is the authoritative game-over signal, so the resolver checks every pick whose `event_start` has passed and skips in-progress games (counted as `not_final`, retried next cron). 1-min cadence means picks usually grade within 1-2 min of the ESPN scoreboard flipping to `post`. UFC ML auto-grades via the ESPN MMA endpoint (`mma/ufc`) using competitor `winner` boolean. UFC SPR/TOT method-of-victory bets stay pending — use the per-row Won/Lost/Push manual settle button on the page. **Visibility**: `resolver_runs` heartbeat table records every invocation (success or crash with full traceback). `/api/handicapper` returns the latest row; `/handicapper` header shows `graded Nm ago · NW/NL` (green) or `STALE` / `CRASHED` (red) so you can tell at a glance if grading is alive.

**Manual settle.** Per-row `✓ Won` / `✗ Lost` / `= Push` buttons on every pending pick. POSTs to `/api/handicapper/pick/<id>/settle`. Same to-WIN PnL math as the resolver. Universal fallback for UFC SPR/TOT, postponed games to void, ESPN-unmatched picks. Confirmation prompt before settling.

**Stat windows.** Three timeframe rows on the stats card: TODAY / LAST 7 DAYS / LAST 30 DAYS. All bucketed by **`event_start`** (today's slate, not "what cron settled today"). "Today" = America/Phoenix calendar day (Arizona, no DST — DON'T use America/Denver, it flips to MDT in summer). Settled list at the bottom is also today-only; yesterday+ rows still drive the rolling stats.

**Routing for in-chat flow**: When the user asks a betting-flavored question about a specific game, invoke the `/handicap` skill (`.claude/skills/handicap.md`).

**Pipeline (in-chat flow)**:
1. `kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper.py "Toronto vs Angels"` — builds a JSON dossier (latest odds across all 14 books, PIN devig + sharp side/score, public splits, ESPN injuries, ESPN team records + last 10, MLB probable pitchers + season stats). Free public sources only — no paid APIs, no Claude API.
2. Claude reads the dossier, applies the strategy rules in `.claude/skills/handicap.md`, writes the full analyst write-up in chat, and proposes a side / market / line / book / price / units / confidence.
3. `kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper_log_pick.py --market-id <uuid> --market-type <ml|spread|total> --side <home|away|over|under> --book DK --price -125 --units 3 --confidence high --analysis-file /tmp/analysis.md --reason "..." --reason "..."` writes the row to `bot_picks`. Idempotent: same `(market_id, market_type, side)` within 7 days is silently skipped (use `--allow-duplicate` to override). If Claude doesn't recommend a pick, the script is just not called.
4. `kahla-scanner/scripts/bot_picks_resolver.py` — appended step in `scanner-poll.yml`. Same ESPN-matching pattern as `paper_bets_resolver.py`, but reads `units` per row for 1/3/5u sizing.

**Pipeline (web flow)**:
1. User types query → page POSTs to `GET /api/handicapper/dossier?q=...` → Flask runs `handicapper_web.build_dossier()` (same shape as the CLI version, ports the math + sharp helpers locally so the kahla-scanner subproject stays out of the Vercel deploy) → JSON returns to the page.
2. Page renders odds grid, splits row, injury blocks, suggestion card.
3. User clicks "Log this pick" → modal pre-fills with the suggestion → POSTs to `/api/handicapper/pick` → row inserted into `bot_picks`.
4. Same resolver grades it.

**Two dossier implementations, kept in sync**:
- `kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper.py` — CLI (uses httpx, kahla-scanner _lib helpers). Authoritative.
- `handicapper_web.py` — Flask-portable port (uses requests, math helpers inlined). When the rules change, change BOTH. The kahla-scanner subproject doesn't ship to Vercel (`vercel.json` only deploys app.py), so Flask can't `import` from it.

**Data sources** (free public, no auth or trivial UA-spoof):
- Supabase `book_snapshots` — odds + PIN opener
- ESPN scoreboard / team injuries / team schedule — `site.api.espn.com` + `site.web.api.espn.com`
- Action Network `api.actionnetwork.com/web/v2/scoreboard/{league}` — public betting splits (same JSON used by `/api/splits`)
- MLB Stats API `statsapi.mlb.com` — probable pitchers + season stats
- ESPN injuries — every supported sport

**Sharp score is recency-weighted PIN movement over the last 18h** (new in May 2026, replaced the legacy "earliest PIN snap of all time" anchor). For each side of each market, `_pin_history` pulls every PIN snapshot in the past 18h. The score is the absolute value of the weighted sum of consecutive deltas, where each delta is multiplied by a recency factor based on how recent its newer endpoint is:

| Age of newer snap | Weight multiplier |
|---|---|
| 0-15 min | **0.75** (was 1.00 — trimmed June 2026) |
| 15-60 min | 0.50 (was 0.60) |
| 1-2 h | 0.35 |
| 2-6 h | 0.18 |
| 6-18 h | 0.08 |
| >18 h | 0.00 (filtered at fetch time) |

Calibration (post-June-2026 trim): a 5c PIN move in the last 15 min scores ~4 (was ~5); a 3c last-tick move now scores ~2 — **below the sharp gate (3), so a marginal last-minute twitch no longer manufactures a "real pick."** The 0-15/15-60 buckets were dropped because a 118-pick review showed last-hour picks underperform (the freshest ticks are often retail noise, not clean steam). Kept MILD so genuine prime-window steam still clears the gate + sizes up; the prime-window SIZING CAP (above) is the precise lever. **The score answers "did sharp money show up RECENTLY?"** — but no longer treats the final tick as gospel. Helpers: `_recency_weight`, `_weighted_signed_delta`, `_weighted_sharp_for_ml/spread/total` in BOTH `handicapper_web.py` (Flask) and `kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper.py` (CLI) — keep verbatim-mirrored. Sharp Bot (`_lib/sharp.py`) is unchanged; that pipeline keeps its own 1-12h opener window for paper-bet picking.

Same direction conventions as the legacy helpers (gotcha #19): ML sharp side = team whose weighted cents-sum is more positive (more favored = harder); SPR sharp side = whose weighted line tightened more (negative line delta); TOT raised → sharp OVER, lowered → sharp UNDER. SPR/TOT keep the "line OR vig, never additive" rule (gotcha #20) — weighted-line move ≥ 0.05 wins; below that, fall back to weighted-vig direction. One-sided PIN snapshots still skip rather than guess (gotcha #21).

**Sizing rubric — quarter-Kelly (May 2026, replaced the fixed thresholds).** Sizing snaps to the 1/3/5u `confidence` chip off a ¼-Kelly stake computed from a signal-derived edge estimate. **1u is the DEFAULT bet** (rev. late May 2026 — standard bankroll discipline: most plays are 1u, 3u/5u are rare). A gated pick is only 3u/5u when the ¼-Kelly stake is genuinely large; otherwise it's a 1u *real pick*. Real-pick-vs-lean is conveyed by `gates_cleared` (the suggestion-card heading + the games-list button color key off it) — NOT by unit size, so a real pick can absolutely be 1u. Lives in `_suggest_picks` / `_kelly_units` in `handicapper_web.py`.

> **Total-side veto + late-window cap (June 2026, from a 118-pick review).** Two guards added after live data showed where the bot leaked:
> - **Total-side veto (`_total_conflict_reason`)** — the TOT side is chosen *purely from PIN line movement*, so pre-fix the bot followed PIN's total down into Coors and bet unders in hitter parks (every Rockies under lost, 4-for-4, −5.84u; MLB unders 13-17 overall vs overs 10-6). The veto **demotes a total pick to a forced lean** (`gates_cleared=False`, so it stops being a real recommendation / one-tap chip) when the OUR-NUMBER read disagrees with the side: a **park veto** (MLB, independent of the model — PF ≥ `_PARK_UNDER_VETO`=104 kills unders, PF ≤ `_PARK_OVER_VETO`=96 kills overs) OR a **model veto** (projected total disagrees by ≥ `_TOTAL_MODEL_VETO_DIFF`=1 run, only when `power.feeds_sizing`). ML/SPR are untouched. **LANDMINE:** the power model still only *nudges sizing* for ML/SPR — this veto is the ONLY place the independent number can override a side for the exchange-follow path. **The veto is DEAD on the live totals path and that's INTENTIONAL (June 2026):** totals run the MODEL-DRIVEN tier (`TOTALS_TEST_MODE=True`), which skips `_total_conflict_reason` by design — on a model-driven side the veto can't help (the side IS the model's pick, and park is already inside `proj_total` via `_park_factor`, so it'd double-count). **The MODEL is the guard, not the veto.** **O/U BLACKLISTED July 4 2026 (user order — `TOTALS_TEST_MODE=False`):** the model-driven totals tier that ran live since June died on its season record — lifetime real book **75-85 / −13.73u**, last 30d 54-63 / −14.19u, **avg CLV ≈ 0** (no edge vs the close), overs and unders bleeding equally (a 0-6 day was the last straw). NO totals suggestions/chips/leans render anywhere; NRFI is a separate market and stays live. Do NOT re-enable the flag — totals return only via a ground-up rebuild that clears the standing earn-in gates (backtest → reference-only → CLV). The model's calls still SHADOW-log to `pickbot_paperlog` (`signal_blob.total_blacklist_shadow`, variant `'totshadow'` — mirrors the retired gate exactly: proj_total+bias vs exchange line, |gap|≥1, MLB, PMM-bid entry, `gates_cleared=false`) so the rebuild has forward data AND the user's fade hypothesis ("just bet against it") is measurable from real rows. **REBUILD ROUND 1 (same day — `scripts/backtest_diamond_totals.py` + `diamond-totals-backtest.yml`): NOT promotable, and the failure mode is now understood as STRUCTURAL.** The Diamond IQ pitcher-spine total (a brand-new engine) reproduced the dead model's disease exactly: +1.16-run over-projection, raw RMSE loses to a naive league mean, 48.8% directional vs the exchange line (n=248; 42% in the 9-10.5 bucket where it disagrees hardest) — ANY totals model built from scoring ingredients out-extremes the market. Two findings survive: (a) the **line-anchored form** `final = line + β·(model−line)` fit β=+0.37 and edged the line-only RMSE 4.30-vs-4.32 held-out — residual signal exists but is far from the >52.4% bar; (b) the **low-total-unders bias corner** appeared for the THIRD independent time (old book +7u at line<8; new model 53.6%/+1.18 toward pick at line<8) — if totals ever return it's that narrow bias bet, not a projection. Future iteration levers: park factors, opponent-adjusted offense, weather — judged by β on the accruing pm_snapshots pairs, never by raw record. The veto only matters if someone ever revives the exchange-follow engine (`TOTALS_SUGGESTIONS_ENABLED`, -10.8u/30d) — don't, without proving it beats the model.
> - **Prime betting window = 60-180min (`_timing_window` + `_PRIME_WINDOW`)** — **extended 2h→3h June 2026 on user conviction** (sharp money moves early — proven 3.5h out on BOS@NYY from snapshot history; the **paperlog** dataset, below, confirms or kills it after 2 weeks). **Only `prime` (60-180min) picks size past 1u via Kelly + glow green**; `late` (<60min, the mushy last hour, 52.9%) and `far` (>180) are **capped at 1u** (caps DOWN only; unknown kickoff → no cap). The proven **+27u HAMMER is still the 90-120 core** (`_is_prime_core` → `signal_blob.prime_core`) so the review can compare the wider 60-180 against it. Windows: `<60` late (amber, capped 1u), `60-180` prime (green glow + size-up; `_isPrime` mirrors in `handicapper.html`; `VERDICT_WINDOW_MS`/`EVAL_WINDOW_MAX`=180 = page chip edge), `>180` far. Picks carry `timing_window`/`prime_core`/`size_capped`/`late_capped` into `signal_blob`; `starts_in_min` threaded via `build_dossier`. **NOTE:** the prime Telegram alert (`/api/handicapper/prime-alert`) fires at the **180-min (3h) top-of-window ENTRY** (constants `_PRIME_LO/HI/BATCH_HI_MIN`=150/180/210) — heads-up as games cross IN, full prime window to act. **Cron cadence NOT extended for this** — at 3h out the dossier rides 15-min PIN data (fine for a conviction bet); the PMM+Kalshi cross-confirm detector (below) is the real freshness fix.
> - **Recency weights trimmed alongside** (`_RECENCY_WEIGHTS`, June 2026): the 0-15min bucket dropped 1.00→0.75 and 15-60min 0.60→0.50, because the freshest ticks were over-trusted as "steam" when last-hour moves are often retail noise. Kept MILD (not gutted) so genuine prime-window steam still scores enough to size up — the pick-time cap above is the precise lever. **Mirror in BOTH `handicapper_web.py` AND `kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper.py`** (gotcha #28).
> - **Unlogged-bet Telegram alert (July 2026 — REPLACED the prime-WINDOW alert)** — the Filled Bot pings when a **recommended bet is showing that the admin hasn't logged**: "go check right now or you'll miss a bet", not "a window opened". Lives in `app.py:_bet_alerts`, called at the end of every `/api/handicapper/paperlog` tick (no separate route/curl — the old `/api/handicapper/prime-alert` endpoint + its `fill_ping` curl are gone; `prime_alerts` table orphaned). Rules: **real gate-cleared bets only** (leans/shadows never alert), in their display window NOW (ML/SPR: per-market `timing_window=='prime'` from this tick's dossier; NRFI: ≥2h30m early window); **suppressed when an ADMIN has a pending `bot_picks` row on the same market GROUP** (ML/SPR are one directional bet — `_alert_mkt_group`; admin uids via Firestore `_admin_uids`, 10-min cache, lookup failure ⇒ no suppression rather than a missed bet); **one ping per bet** (marker `signal_blob.bet_alerted` ISO stamp on the bet's latest REAL paperlog row — a side/units change inserts a new row and earns a fresh alert; shadows filtered via `_is_shadow_blob` so they can't mask the marker); **batched, max ONE message per 5 min** (`_BET_ALERT_COOLDOWN_S`, cooldown read from the same markers — throttled candidates stay unmarked and retry next tick); quiet before 7am AZ. Coverage note: the paperlog processes a budgeted random subset of games per tick, so a bet is alerted within a few ticks of appearing, not always the same minute.
| Conf | Units | When |
|---|---|---|
| low | 1u | **Default.** Either a forced lean (gate not cleared, sharp < 3) OR a real gated pick whose ¼-Kelly stake < `KELLY_MED_PCT` (2.0% bankroll). ~90% of picks. |
| medium | 3u | **Top tier (5u disabled June 2026, user call).** Gate cleared AND ¼-Kelly stake ≥ `KELLY_MED_PCT` (2.0%) AND `sharp_score ≥ SHARP_FOR_3U` (4) AND `market_type ∈ SIZE_UP_MARKETS` ({moneyline}). Uncommon. |
| ~~high~~ | ~~5u~~ | **DISABLED June 2026** — `_suggest_picks` clamps `units>3 → 3u`. The 5u tier carried too much variance for the edge; ceiling is 3u. `SHARP_FOR_5U` constant kept but unused. |

> **Before this rev every gated pick floored at 3u** (single 2.5% threshold, no 1u-for-gated path), so the bot never emitted a 1u recommendation — wrong for unit-based betting where 1u is the workhorse. The fix: 1u is the default for a gated pick; `KELLY_MED_PCT`/`KELLY_HIGH_PCT` are the step-ups.

The edge estimate (`edge_pp`, which now finally populates the long-nullable `bot_picks.edge_pp` column) = `sharp_score × 0.40pp` + `aligned_splits_pp × 0.10pp` + a power-rating confirmation nudge (capped 1.5pp via `MODEL_FEEDS_SIZING = True` + `MODEL_EDGE_CAP_PP`) + an **order-book pressure nudge** (June 2026, per user: ≥1.3× more contracts bid than offered on the picked side = demand sellers aren't meeting = the line is about to move; `_book_pressure` in `handicapper_web.py`, capped 1pp via `BOOK_PRESSURE_CAP_PP` — the signal predicts the NEXT TICK (~1¢ ≈ 1pp), not the game outcome, so don't raise the cap — bid side must also clear `BOOK_PRESSURE_MIN_Q`=500 contracts (thin-book noise/spoof floor), fetched only for FINAL gated picks — one PMM book call each — recorded as `signal_blob.book_imb` for the paperlog review; synthetic NO sides read the inverted ladder), hard-capped at 6pp. The power rating is the bot's INDEPENDENT number — the whole point of not just echoing PIN — so it DOES feed sizing as a capped nudge when it agrees with the sharp side; the cap keeps the crude v1 from doing damage while the real opponent-adjusted, pitcher-aware engine is built (see the power-ratings pipeline below), and CLV measures whether its calls beat the close so we can widen the cap as it proves out. `_kelly_units` converts that to a full-Kelly fraction at the entry odds, takes a quarter of it (`KELLY_FRACTION = 0.25` — survives variance), and buckets it. **The coefficients are a conservative provisional guess; the new `clv_pp` column measures the bot's REALIZED edge so Stage-4 self-tuning can replace them with calibrated numbers.** The sharp gate (`SHARP_SCORE_MIN = 3`, lowered from 4 in May 2026 — at 4 the recency-weighted score left most of the slate as grey leans) decides real-pick (blue) vs forced-lean (grey); Kelly then sizes among gated picks (1u default → 3u top). **Size-up to 3u additionally requires `sharp_score ≥ SHARP_FOR_3U` (4) and `market_type=moneyline` — see the "Sizing up is gated TWO ways" note above.** 5u and whale (10u) are both disabled.

**Four signals added May 2026 (all free — zero new Odds API calls, zero new paid services):**
1. **CLV on picks** (`clv_pp` column, migration 007). The resolver (`bot_picks_resolver.py:_compute_clv`) pulls PIN's last pre-`event_start` snapshot on both sides of the pick's market from `book_snapshots`, devigs the pair, and computes `(closing_devig_prob_for_side − entry_implied_prob) × 100`. Positive = the bot was early on the side the line later moved toward (sharp). Mirrors `app.py:_clv_pin_close_pair`. `/api/handicapper` rolls up `avg_clv_pp` per timeframe bucket (TODAY / 7d / 30d / per-confidence) and returns `clv_pp` on each settled row; the page shows a CLV stat cell + a per-row CLV chip. **CLV is the edge-proof metric** — a positive average proves +EV in ~100 picks vs the 1000+ that hit-rate needs to stabilize. Computed once at grade time (closing line is fixed at `event_start`, never recomputed); stays NULL when PIN has no closing pair.
2. **Kelly sizing** — see the rubric above.
3. **Weather** (`dossier.weather`, `handicapper_web.py:_fetch_weather`). Free Open-Meteo (no key/signup/quota) keyed off a static MLB-park / NFL-stadium lat-lon table (`_MLB_PARKS` / `_NFL_STADIUMS`). Climate-controlled domes / fixed-roof venues are flagged `dome=True` and skip the fetch. Indoor sports (NBA/NHL/CBB) and the un-enumerable NCAAF venue list are skipped entirely. Reports temp / wind (speed + compass dir) / precip / sky near game time as a **reference card** — NOT auto-sized (wind-out-to-CF needs park orientation we don't encode yet). 30-min in-module cache.
4. **Power rating** (`dossier.power_rating`, `handicapper_web.py:_power_rating`). An OUR-number-vs-the-market check built from the team-comparison stats the dossier ALREADY fetches (zero extra calls). Projects a margin from offense-vs-defense season averages (`_POWER_MODELS` per sport: MLB/NBA/CBB/NFL/NCAAF/NHL), converts to a win prob via a per-sport logistic, derives model fair lines, and compares to PIN's devigged fair (`edge_{home,away}_pp`) + a total lean. **v1 is crude** (no SOS/Elo, and MLB uses team-season ERA — blind to the starting pitcher), so its sizing contribution is capped at 1.5pp via `MODEL_FEEDS_SIZING = True` + `MODEL_EDGE_CAP_PP`. It IS the bot's independent number (the whole point of not just echoing PIN), surfaced as an `OUR NUMBER` reference card + a `_buildReasons` bullet, and it feeds the Kelly edge as a capped confirmation nudge when it agrees with the sharp side. **The real engine is being built** — see "Power-ratings pipeline (real model)" below — which replaces the raw season averages with opponent-adjusted, recency-weighted, pitcher-aware projections; as CLV validates it (bucket CLV by model-agree vs model-disagree), we widen the cap. All four are web-only (`handicapper_web.py`) — the CLI dossier doesn't have suggestion/team-compare logic, so this doesn't violate the dossier-mirror rule (the verbatim-shared sharp-score helpers are untouched).

> **Whale (10u) tier was disabled May 11 2026.** Live results showed
> `sharp ≥ 7 AND splits ≥ 10pp aligned` hitting 23% over 35 picks
> (~3 std devs below random) for -116.73u, while the high (5u) tier
> hit 57% over 35 picks for +30.96u. The criteria turned out to be a
> *fade* indicator in MLB — the market has already steamed both
> signals by the time the bot fires them. Top sizing capped at 5u
> until a higher tier can be shown to outperform. `bot_picks`
> rows tagged `confidence='whale'` from before this date are
> historical only; new picks never get the tier.

> **Sizing up (3u/5u) is gated TWO ways now (June 2026 — 365-pick live review). Both live in the `_suggest_picks` sizing loop in `handicapper_web.py`.**
> 1. **Earned by SHARP STEAM, not by `edge_pp` pegging at its cap (`SHARP_FOR_3U`=4 / `SHARP_FOR_5U`=6).** The bug: Kelly sizes off `edge_pp`, but `edge_pp` hits its 6pp hard cap from VSiN splits + the crude power model even when `sharp_score` is weak — so sharp-light picks got sized to 5u (smoking gun: a Pirates 5u ML rode `sharp_score=2.0`, `edge_pp` pegged at 6.0, lost -5.15u; `sharp=2.0` is below the gate of 3 and only stayed a "real pick" via the sticky/hysteresis gate, which exists for UI chip-stability, NOT conviction). Sized-up (3u+) PnL by `sharp_score`: **≤3 → 4-5, -7.5u** (pure bleed, where decayed sticky picks live); **4-5 → 13-14, +1.6u** (breakeven, +1.9 CLV); **6+ → 11-9, +14.1u** (the real edge, +2.5 CLV). So 3u needs `sharp≥4`, 5u needs `sharp≥6`, else cap 1u. This also defuses the sticky path: a sticky score (≥1.5 to stay real) is < `SHARP_FOR_3U`, so hysteresis keeps the chip stable but can't size up.
> 2. **MONEYLINE-ONLY size-up (`SIZE_UP_MARKETS={"moneyline"}`).** Sized-up by market: **ML 19-12 +27.4u, TOT 9-11 -7.2u, SPR 0-10 (0%!) -12.0u** — spreads never won a sized-up bet, ever. Kelly is real for ML, pure variance on spreads (leveraged restatement of the same directional bet) and totals (sized off line movement alone; the total-side veto already flags those leaks). SPR + TOT cap at 1u. Replaced the older totals-capped-at-3u guard. Sharp-gating alone does NOT save spreads (a `sharp=10` spread still lost -3.48u with +20 CLV), so this market cap is independently needed.
>
> Both are **bankroll defense, not model changes** — SPR/TOT and sharp-light picks are still bet, just at 1u. Same whale-tier lesson (size on sharp steam, never on splits/model alone), at the sizing layer. Re-check before loosening either: `select case when sharp_score>=6 then '6+' when sharp_score>=4 then '4-5' else '<4' end b, market_type, count(*), sum(pnl_units) from bot_picks where units>=3 group by 1,2`.

**Schema** (`kahla-scanner/supabase/bot_picks.sql`): one row per pick. Columns include `units` (1/3/5), `confidence` (low/medium/high/max), `analysis_md` (full write-up rendered on the page), `reasons` (jsonb array of bullet reasons), plus the standard market/entry/resolution fields. Run manually in Supabase SQL editor.

**Backtest**: `kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper_backtest.py --sport MLB --days 30 --cutoff-min 30 --edge-min 1.0 --sharp-min 4` — replays historical `book_snapshots` at a cutoff time, picks via rule-based logic (no Claude involvement), grades via ESPN. **Limitation**: ESPN injuries, splits, and lineups are not historical via free APIs — backtest is signal-only (PIN move + edge). Useful as a calibration check; a positive ROI from rule-based alone validates the live picks are at least starting from a non-negative baseline.

### Market-anchored spread model (SHADOW — MLB, June 2026)

The thesis the live data forced (see also the ML/SPR/TOT "which question does the market price" framing): **the exchange is the sharpest signal for WHO WINS (ML), but a spread = that same direction + a margin dimension the winner-market doesn't price.** So a spread fair = **market direction × model margin**: anchor a joint *(home runs, away runs)* distribution to the **exchange ML devigged win prob**, let the **run-total model (`power.proj_total`)** supply the magnitude, and read the runline cover prob off the joint. Runs are **negative-binomial per team** (`SPREAD_RUN_VAR_MEAN`≈2.1 — MLB team runs are overdispersed vs Poisson). Implementation in `handicapper_web.py`: `_nb_run_pmf` → `_spread_winprob` → `_spread_cover_prob` (bisects λ_home+λ_away=proj_total so P(home wins)==market win prob, then sums the joint past the runline) → `_spread_model_block` (per-side cover prob + edge vs the exchange's *own* devigged spread price + the +EV side if edge ≥ `SPREAD_MODEL_MIN_EDGE_PP`=4pp). Sanity-checked: a pickem −1.5 fairs ~35% (matches real runline prices), low total → harder to cover −1.5, high total → easier, and the solved λ-split reproduces the input win prob exactly.

**Why this engine exists:** spread STEAM is just noisy ML (sized-up spreads went **0-for-10**), so spreads can't ride exchange movement — but they CAN ride the exchange's *direction* (which IS sharp) plus a model margin. The OLD power-model spread used the model for *direction* too (the model's weakest dimension — 45.6% on MLB ML sides), which is backwards; this anchors direction to the market and only models the margin.

**Rollout = SHADOW (the totals playbook).** `build_dossier` attaches `spread_model` (`SPREAD_MODEL_MODE`), the paperlog logs the +EV side at flat 1u flagged `signal_blob.spread_model=true`, and Details shows a "Spread — market-anchored model" card — but it is **NOT a user-facing card pick yet** (kept isolated from the validated `_suggest_picks` ML/SPR/TOT machinery on purpose; the existing steam-spread path is untouched). Validate ~2 weeks via the paperlog (`select … from pickbot_paperlog where market_type='spread' and signal_blob->>'spread_model'='true'`), discover its prime window from `starts_in_min` like NRFI/TOT, then promote (wire into `_suggest_picks`, real units, card chip). MLB-only (the run model is MLB). Tune `SPREAD_RUN_VAR_MEAN` from the realized margin distribution once data accrues. **First honest read (July 3, post-dedup): 16-13, −1.7u, avg CLV −9.2pp — NOT promotable.** Two structural warts to fix before trusting it: (a) it picks **home +1.5 almost every time** (141/144 pre-dedup rows) — the NB joint systematically finds "edge" on the home dog-line, which smells like model bias (bottom-of-9th truncation shrinks home win margins, so the independent-NB joint overprices home blowouts), not market error; (b) the uniformly negative CLV on +1.5 rows vs uniformly POSITIVE on the steam spreads' −1.5 rows suggests `_compute_clv`'s closing pair may not be line-aware for the mirrored side. Don't promote on raw aggregates — the pre-July data was also duplicate-inflated (see the paperlog LANDMINE).

### NRFI / YRFI (first-inning model — MLB only, OUR-NUMBER prop)

The Pick Bot's first **derivative** market. NRFI = No Run First Inning (neither team scores in the 1st); YRFI = a run scores top or bottom of the 1st. Web-only (like the power model) — **not mirrored into the CLI dossier** (`kahla-scanner/scripts/handicapper.py`). Lives in `handicapper_web.py:_nrfi_model`, surfaced as a dedicated "First inning — NRFI / YRFI" card on `/handicapper` with `logNrfi('no'|'yes')` buttons.

**The model — why it's NOT a scaled game total:** two structural facts drive it. (1) The first inning **always faces the top of the order** (hitters 1-2-3), so the offense input is **top-of-order OBP**, not team RPG — a mediocre team with a monster 1-2-3 has a high YRFI. (2) It's **two independent half-innings vs two different pitchers**, so `P(NRFI) = q_top · q_bot` (top: away bats vs home SP; bottom: home bats vs away SP). Each `q` (scoreless-half prob) maps an expected-first-inning-runs index `xr` through a **calibrated logistic — deliberately NOT raw Poisson** (half-inning scoring is zero-inflated/bursty; Poisson(λ≈0.53) overpredicts → ~63% YRFI vs the real ~47-50%). Anchored so league-average inputs land at `NRFI_Q_BASE` (**0.713 → NRFI ≈ 0.508**); slope `NRFI_Q_SLOPE` is backtest-tuned. `xr = NRFI_XR0 · (top_order_obp/LG_OBP) · (opposing_sp_ra9/LG_RA9) · (park/100) · temp_nudge`. SP run-prevention reuses `_starter_runs` (FIP/ERA blend, IP-regressed); top-of-order OBP comes from the **posted lineup's 1/2/3 hitters** (boxscore `seasonStats`) when posted, else **team OBP × 1.10** fallback. Wind is reference-only (no park orientation encoded — same stance as the power model's weather layer). Current anchor: league-avg → 50.8%; two aces + weak bats → 60% NRFI; two scrubs + hot bats → 41% (YRFI lean). **Calibrated against a 796-game 2026 backtest (May 2026):** observed NRFI base rate 48.6% (offense up vs the historical ~52.5%), Brier 0.2475 beat the 0.2498 base-rate baseline, monotonic calibration table — model has weak-but-real discrimination. `NRFI_Q_BASE` was dropped 0.725→0.713 to recenter the model mean (was +1.7pp biased toward NRFI) onto the observed 48.6%; the slope held (tail bins too thin to retune). The market prices the current run environment, so we match it, not the historical rate.

**Bet determination = model fair vs the POLYMARKET line.** There's no NRFI line in our Odds-API feed, so `pmm_markets.lookup` pulls Polymarket's first-inning market (`_classify_first_inning` sniffs it by question text — it lives OUTSIDE the ml/spread/total buckets; returns it as `pmm_data["nrfi"]`). `_nrfi_model` then computes the **edge per side = model_fair_prob − PMM maker-entry (bid) prob**; the +EV side with edge in the **2.5-6pp band** (`NRFI_EDGE_MIN_PP`/`NRFI_EDGE_MAX_PP` — the 6pp clamp added July 2026: on 247 graded paperlog rows the edge was real at 3-6pp, YRFI +21.5u/151, while claimed edges past 6pp graded flat on both sides — at that distance the market is right, not the model; **floor lowered 3.0→2.5 Aug 2 2026 (user call) as an EARN-IN — the [2.5,3.0) bucket was never measurable before (nothing below 3 ever logged), grade it via paperlog `edge_pp` after ~50 rows and restore 3.0 if it doesn't print**) is THE bet, AND its entry must clear the **60¢ price cap** (`NRFI_MAX_ENTRY_C`, user guardrail Aug 2 2026 — never suggest/place a Y/NRFI entry above 60¢ whatever the edge, the Coors-YRFI-at-58¢ case; mirrored in the re-peg engine as `_REPEG_NRFI_PRICE_CAP_C` so a chase can't cross it) — its button lights up green and `entry_price` = the PMM bid (maker-only entry, like the other markets). No edge → pass; **no PMM market → the KALSHI NRFI top-of-book prices the edge instead** (July 2026 fix — `_nrfi_model` late-imports `app._kalshi_nrfi_book`, the SAME reader the card's make/take chip uses, so the card can no longer show a live Kalshi quote next to "no exchange 1st-inning market found"; each `polymarket[side]` block + the top-level `price_src` carry `'polymarket'`/`'kalshi'`, paperlog rows record `signal_blob.nrfi_price_src`). Only when BOTH venues miss → model fair shown for reference, no bet button. **Bovada book line (July 24 2026):** `nrfi.book` carries Bovada's 1st-inning "run scored" Yes/No quote (`app.py:_bovada_nrfi_quote` — public event JSON, no auth, coupon 10-min + detail 5-min caches, nearest-start match guard) — rendered on the card ("Bovada book: YRFI +110 · NRFI −145") and stamped into paperlog NRFI rows as `signal_blob.book_nrfi` (the exchange-vs-book lag dataset). It exists because the user's Poly makers don't always fill and they want a retail fallback + a slow-book shopping metric. **Don't re-run the vendor hunt: NO odds API sells the 1st-inning market** (ParlayAPI + The Odds API both stop at 1st-5-innings — verified against ParlayAPI's live `/v1/meta/markets` July 2026; SportsGameOdds has it but starts at $99/mo), and **DraftKings hard-403s Vercel IPs** (Akamai). Verify/iterate via `/debug-dk-nrfi` (`?src=bovada&cat=drill` shape dump, `?src=quote&away=..&home=..&start=<iso>` production-reader check). The model-vs-baseline `lean` is kept as secondary context only. **PMM's NRFI schema is UNVERIFIED from the sandbox** — `lookup`'s `diag.all_markets` dumps every market PMM returns for the event (surfaced via Copy-for-Claude → `pmm_meta.diag`) so we can confirm/tune `_classify_first_inning` against a real game. **Sizing: flat 1u** (`confidence='low'`) — the 0.5u restriction was lifted June 2026 (user call: NRFI grades cleanly off the 1st-inning linescore and runs +EV — 30-24 lifetime, no stuck-pending). Was 0.25u → 0.5u → now 1u; still flat (no Kelly, no sharp signal to size on) while the model calibrates. `entry_line` is NULL. The unit size is wired in `templates/handicapper.html` (`logNrfi` seed + the line-2 chip body + the dossier NRFI card), NOT in the model — change it there if you re-tune. Grading is automatic and unchanged (`bot_picks_resolver._grade_nrfi`, 1st-inning linescore, bypasses the post gate).

**Grading is fully automatic + clean** (unlike UFC method bets): `bot_picks_resolver.py` reads ESPN's **first-inning linescore** (`competitors[].linescores[0].value`) — any run in inning 1 → YRFI. NRFI grading **bypasses the post gate** and can settle the moment the 1st inning completes (`state=='post'` OR `period >= 2`); YRFI resolves the instant either half posts a run. No CLV (no NRFI closing line in `book_snapshots`).

**Schema:** market_type `'nrfi'`, side `'yes'`(YRFI)/`'no'`(NRFI) — added by `bot_picks_migrations/008_nrfi.sql` (alters the market_type + side CHECK constraints; **already applied to the live DB**). `app.py:/api/handicapper/pick` validates nrfi→yes/no. `entry_line` NULL.

**Backtest:** `kahla-scanner/scripts/nrfi_backtest.py` (+ `.github/workflows/nrfi-backtest.yml`, `workflow_dispatch`). Pulls completed MLB games from the MLB Stats API (hydrates 1st-inning linescore + probables in one call/date), predicts P(NRFI), reports base rate / model mean / Brier / accuracy / calibration table. **Limitation (documented in the script):** uses current-season cumulative stats = mild lookahead leakage, and team-OBP fallback (no historical lineup) — it's a **calibration/anchor check on the model FORM, not a clean walk-forward edge proof**. Tune `NRFI_Q_BASE`/`NRFI_Q_SLOPE` in **both** `handicapper_web.py` AND `nrfi_backtest.py` (constants mirrored on purpose — keep in sync). v1 is reference; let live results prove the edge.

### Prop Bets pipeline (v1 — July 26 2026, user: "time to start working props before football season")

Generic exchange PROPS (player props, first-five-innings, quarters/halves/periods, team props — everything Polymarket lists on a game event that isn't a full-game main line, the NRFI market, or the UFC distance prop). Thesis (user): "Markets move the same, we can log the same" — props are priced order books like the main lines, so the SAME movement engine applies. **v1 is Polymarket-only; Kalshi props are the explicit next step** (the `venue` column exists everywhere for it — verify series tickers via `/debug-kalshi-discover?q=...` first, the Fight IQ pattern; KXUFCROUNDS/KXUFCMOV/KXUFCDISTANCE are known, MLB/NFL player-prop series are not).

- **Ingest = FREE.** `pmm_markets.lookup()` now returns a `props` list (capped `_PROPS_CAP`=80/event) — collected from the markets the main-line classifier already fetched and dropped, so prop ingestion rides the existing per-game lookup at zero extra network. `_pmm_game_quotes` passes them through; the **pm-snapshot tick** writes `prop_snapshots` (cent history per (game, venue, prop_key=PMM slug), deduped on change — `_prop_insert_changed`) and upserts `prop_suggestions` (the current movement verdict per prop — `_prop_update_suggestions`). DDL `kahla-scanner/supabase/prop_snapshots.sql` (both tables, **applied to the live DB July 26 2026**).
- **Suggestion = the movement engine.** Recency-weighted signed sum of consecutive cent deltas over 18h (`_PROP_RECENCY` mirrors the main sharp-score buckets; cents ARE prob points here, no scale constant). Side = harder-=-sharp: YES price rising → money arriving on YES → suggest `yes`; falling → `no`. `cleared` = weighted move ≥ `_PROP_SUGGEST_MIN` (3.0¢) AND current price inside 4-96¢ (no near-resolved longshots), with **hysteresis** (`_PROP_SUGGEST_EXIT`=2.0 — a cleared prop stays cleared until its score decays below 2.0 on the same side; without it, gate-hovering scores flickered the badge's ▲ count against the card tick-to-tick).
- **UI.** Games-list rows show a **`Prop Bets X` badge** next to the matchup — **X = prop MARKETS listed on the game (the pull), not just movers** (user call July 26: "even with no bets, the pull should show"); a green tint + `· N▲` marks the movement-cleared subset. Batch-queried in `/api/handicapper/games` from `prop_suggestions` (6h freshness bound so a dead feed ages out; taps open the dossier). The dossier has a **Prop Bets card** — async-loaded from **`GET /api/handicapper/props?market_id=` (`@bot_required`,** live PMM lookup merged with the suggestion scores): cleared movers on top with a green Log button for the moved side, full board behind "Show all N".
- **Logging + grading.** `market_type='prop'`, side yes/no (migration `015_prop.sql`, **applied**); entry = the maker BID of the chosen side (yes bid / inverted no bid), flat 1u/low seed. The prop's identity travels in `signal_blob.prop` (`{key, q, venue, type, line, move_score, move_side, suggested}`) and **`query_text` carries the QUESTION** — that's how the pending list shows which prop the row is. **LANDMINE: the 7-day dedup can't tell two props on the same game apart** (`(market_id,'prop',side)` collides across different props), so prop logs MUST send `allow_duplicate` (the modal always does). **Grading is MANUAL** — the resolver explicitly skips `prop` rows (`unsupported`, stays pending; ESPN's scoreboard can't grade player props). Auto-grade via the exchange's own market resolution is the phase-2 upgrade to build alongside Kalshi props.
- **No sizing model on the movement flags** — a prop suggestion is a movement flag, not a Kelly-sized pick. Earn-in path is the standard one: accrue suggestions + logged results, review, then decide whether prop movement predicts (the VSiN/model precedent: measure before it sizes anything).
- **Whiff IQ (Aug 3 2026) — the first MODEL-priced prop, pitcher strikeouts, SHADOW-ONLY.** Engine `_lib/whiff_iq.py` (leash = decay-weighted BF, log5 matchup K-rate pitcher×opp-lineup×league, binomial tail mixed over the leash spread) — walk-forward (3,363 held-out 2026 starts, `backtest_whiff_iq.py`/`whiff-iq-backtest.yml`) **beats base rate at EVERY rung K≥3..8** (e.g. K≥6 Brier 0.2046 vs 0.2300); calibration is compressed toward the middle (reality steeper than model — a sharpening recal is the v2 lever). **Market tape verdict (610 graded props, one week):** favorite-longshot bias — K≥6/7 YES overpriced +8-9pp, low rungs underpriced; likely STRUCTURAL: **the Polymarket app sells props YES-ONLY (user-discovered — retail can't take the other side), but the API can: `BUY_SHORT` on a K-prop probe-proven Aug 3** (order `BN37TJQEWBA1`, create/cancel clean; same landmines as game markets — retrieve 404s, modify→REPLACED; **write prices are YES-CANONICAL: NO at 3¢ = price 0.97 — the probe-exec guard enforces off-touch**). Live wiring: daily `compute_whiff_iq.py` (`whiff-iq-compute.yml` 10:55 UTC) serializes name-keyed state → `whiff_iq_snapshot` (DDL applied; ambiguous duplicate names DROPPED); `app.py:_whiff_shadow_pass` on the pm-snapshot tick prices every captured K-prop (`_whiff_tail_p` MIRRORS the engine — verified exact; ≥4pp edge, ≥3 starts, side-change dedup) → paperlog shadows `signal_blob.whiff_shadow` + **variant `'whiff:'+whiff_key` (key-scoped — many K props share one game, a bare variant collides: the June-28-flood rule)**; graded daily by `grade_whiff_paperlog.py` from `mlb_pitcher_games` (name+ET-date match; DH double-starts skipped — it grades BOTH the paperlog shadows AND the real `bot_picks` prop rows, the ONLY grader for either: the per-minute resolver skips `prop`). **LIVE AUTO-BET same day (user: "why shadow, model checks, let's go... limit to 30"):** `_whiff_autobet` places 1-contract post-only makers on the MODEL's side (usually NO — the API-exclusive side), **best rung per PITCHER only** (ladder rungs of one start are correlated — never stack), edge band `_WHIFF_BET_MIN/MAX_PP`=4-10pp (**>10pp claims stay shadow-only — the third-time claimed-edge-cliff rule**), entry ≤60¢ (`_WHIFF_MAX_ENTRY_C`, kills the -400 chalk automatically), peg = side-mid −2¢ (post-only: a crossing peg REJECTS → retries next tick), own slate cap `WHIFF_AUTOBET_MAX_BETS`=30 via `cap_flag='whiff_autobet'` (does NOT eat the opener lane's 40; `_autobet_execute` grew cap_flag/cap_max/skip_game_dedup params — prop lanes dedup per-pitcher, a game carries 2 starter bets), ≤1 create/tick (pm-snapshot latency), **and a DAY-OF placement window (`_WHIFF_DAYOF_MIN`=360, user-shipped Aug 9): the first make order waits until T-6h — the reward schedule has NO props Early program (pre-T-6h resting earns zero), the overnight rest is the uncompensated scratch-option window (fill-times retro: slow fills 41% vs 54% fast, no time-before-start win effect), and T-6h caps bid staleness. Shadows still log from listing; too-early candidates retry each tick.** **Props RE-PEG like Y/NRFI (same day):** `_REPEG_MARKET_TYPES` includes `prop`; whiff bets chase under the model-is-the-wall regime (`is_model_bet` = autobet OR whiff_autobet) with the **whiff 4pp floor** (not NRFI's 2.5pp chase floor), the 60¢ cap, and per-pitcher fair from the pick's stamped `fair_prob` (the NRFI paperlog fair fallback is explicitly blocked for prop rows — it's a GAME-level number). Manual/user-logged props never chase.

- **Outs IQ (Aug 5 2026 night — "we leaving money on the table... Nah, fuck that, let's go!"): the SECOND pitcher-prop family, LIVE on the whiff machinery same-night.** Gates cleared in ~40 min: walk-forward (`backtest_outs_iq.py`/`outs-iq-backtest.yml`, 3,074 eval starts) beats base rate at 7/8 lines peaking at the quoted 16-18 (+.013 Brier); market check (one SQL over our own prop_snapshots tape, 699 settled props) found **outs-YES underpriced +6-15pp across the 44-73¢ band**. Model = per-pitcher decay-weighted outs mean/SD shrunk 4 starts to league, normal tail (`_outs_tail_p` MIRRORS the backtest — keep in sync). Snapshot hist rows are `[date,bf,k,OUTS]` (4th element, K path slices [0:3]); lg carries `outs_m/outs_s` — an old snapshot without them = quiet no-op. Same gates as K (4-10pp band, 25-60¢, 0.30-0.70 trust, ≥3 starts) **enforced at the PEG too** (first live bet pegged 12¢/31pp on a wide fresh book — the Wesneski shape's third sighting; `_whiff_autobet` now re-checks floor+cliff at the resting entry). K and outs candidates share ONE per-pitcher pool (correlated — one bet per pitcher, best edge across both ladders) and the whiff_autobet cap family, so harvest/repeg/sweep/watchdog apply unchanged. Grader picks the metric off `whiff.ptype`. **Hits-allowed + walks-allowed LIVE next night (Aug 6)** via the generalized `_PSTAT_FAMS` config (ptype/regex/hist-idx/lg-keys/SD-floor per family; hist rows now `[date,bf,k,outs,hits,walks]`): gate 1 hits 7/7 lines, walks 4/4 (+.0083 at the quoted 2-line); walks also has IN-BAND market bias (+7.4pp at 50¢, n=179). **EARNED RUNS FAILED its backtest (sequencing luck — the run-projection disease) and deliberately has NO family; don't add it without a new engine.** PMM listing cadence gotcha: outs ladders post the NIGHT BEFORE, hits/walks ladders post GAME DAY — an evening with zero ha/bb captures is normal. **BATTER TOTAL BASES — backtested Aug 6, deliberately NOT wired** (`backtest_batter_tb.py`/`batter-tb-backtest.yml`, 29,737 held-out batter-games, decayed empirical per-batter rate shrunk to league): gate 1 passes but THIN — beats base rate at all three lines by only +.0009 to +.0012, 5-10× weaker than the pitcher families (one batter's game is four random PAs). Gate 2 says the value seat is **NO** (retail overbuys TB-YES by 3.5-5pp), but NO prices at 56-85¢ — **above the 60¢ entry cap**, so wiring it needs a policy call on raising the cap for short-side props. ALSO BLOCKED ON A BUG: captured TB questions name players who aren't in the stated game ("Pete Alonso… in BAL vs TEX"), so PMM groups batter props under odd events or our capture mis-associates them — harmless for analysis (we grade by name+date) but live orders inherit their game's GTD/dedup/sweep, so fix the event association before betting. Remaining queue after that: football props at Week 1.

### Polymarket trade tape — whale flow (v1, July 26 2026)

The cron-friendly answer to enviodev/track-poly-trades (user rejected the Envio/HyperSync blockchain-streaming path — always-on Bun process, new paid dep — in favor of polling **Polymarket's public data API tape**: same information, zero new infra). **ONE poll of `data-api.polymarket.com/trades` per pm-snapshot tick** (`app.py:_poly_trades_ingest`, ~1-2s I/O): big TAKER fills only (`_TRADE_MIN_USD`=500 notional), matched to OUR games — upcoming PLUS started-within-7h, so in-play flow lands on the LIVE game — by mascot last-tokens OR Kalshi-tricode slug tokens (`_match_poly_trade`, unit-tested). **MATCHER LANDMINES (July 26 SEA@TEX incident — today's in-play 88-99¢ trades landed on TOMORROW's row):** the event-slug DATE is a **VETO** (not a tie-break — a sole candidate with the wrong ET date is dropped), `" AND "` parlay titles are skipped, candidates spanning different matchups = ambiguous-skip, and a dateless same-series tie goes to the game nearest the TRADE time. Insert-new-only into `poly_trades` (sha1 `trade_key` unique; DDL `kahla-scanner/supabase/poly_trades.sql`, **applied July 26 2026**; 15d retention via the nightly cleanup). **VENUE CAVEAT: this is the GLOBAL polymarket.com book's tape — an order-flow SIGNAL (who's hammering a side before the price moves), NOT a record of the user's own US-exchange orders.** Surface: the dossier's **Big Trades card** (`handicapper_web._big_trades` — top-by-notional last 24h, newest-first; renders only when rows exist). **Earn-in: recorded + shown, NOT a scoring/sizing input** — reviews join `poly_trades` → `pickbot_paperlog` on (market_id, time window). **First graded read (July 28, 128 trades ≥$10k vs finals): SIZE ALONE IS NOT SHARP — pre-game whale money went 33-46, −15.8% ROI (totals −26.5%), while in-play ran flat.** But the aggregate hides a wallet split: one account was −$386k of the damage (11-21) while others ran 9-1/+$107k and 4-0/+$158k — so the review question is WALLET PERSISTENCE (follow/fade specific accounts), not size-following (user decision: keep stacking per-wallet data, decide usage later). Grade by joining `poly_trades` → `game_results` on (ET date, teams); parse ML/TOT/SPR from `title`, skip first-five/inning rows; SELL ≈ opening short (approximation). **The tape API's schema was UNVERIFIED at build time** — the reader tries 3 param shapes with tolerant field names; **`/debug-poly-trades`** (public, no secrets) dumps attempt statuses + raw/normalized samples + unmatched titles — hit it FIRST if `poly_trades` stays empty (403 = the DK-Akamai class of problem; empty attempts = param-shape drift).

### Fight IQ — the UFC model (Phase 1 DONE July 2026)

The phased UFC model (plan artifact "Fight IQ", July 2026). Targeting: **the DURATION family is the flagship — the distance prop first, the rounds ladder with it** (the NRFI playbook — derivative market, model-priced vs PMM + Kalshi `KXUFCROUNDS`, mechanical grading; weight-class finish rates measured from our own table run LHW 62.8% -> BW 43.8% post-2020), **winner model starts on VETO duty** (sentiment fights — McGregor archetype), method props later. Same gates as football: walk-forward backtest -> reference-only live -> CLV earn-in.

**Phase 1 (DONE): the data spine.** `ufc_fighters` + `ufc_fights` (DDL `kahla-scanner/supabase/ufc_stats.sql`, applied): **8,758 bouts / 779 events (1994 -> present)** with result/method/round/time/weight-class, and career rates (SLpM, TD avg/acc/def, sub avg, reach, stance, DOB) for **100% of fighters who have a UFC bout** (undetailed roster rows = never-fought signees, irrelevant). Scraped by `kahla-scanner/scripts/ingest_ufc_stats.py` via `.github/workflows/ufc-stats-ingest.yml` (modes probe/backfill/delta; weekly Mon 10:00 UTC delta keeps it current). **LANDMINES (each cost a run):** (a) UFCStats fronts requests with a **JS proof-of-work challenge** — the Fetcher solves it in headless Chromium and reuses the clearance cookie; **Playwright is installed by the workflow step ONLY, never requirements.txt** (scanner-poll pip-installs that file every minute); (b) parsers are **class-independent** (anchored on event-/fight-/fighter-details hrefs — the site's CSS classes don't match older references); (c) **Supabase's default 1,000-row cap** silently truncated the null-detail query — explicit `.limit()` on any full-table scan; (d) the first cold browser navigation is the flakiest fetch of a run — the browser path retries and an empty events index aborts LOUD (a silent empty once skipped the entire events phase while fighters kept flowing).

**Phase 2 (DONE): the engine.** `_lib/ufc_model.py` — method-weighted FightElo + layers (age curve past 32 @ 12pts/yr, chin = KO losses in last 2, layoff tiers 30/60pts, TD-offense x TD-defense style clash, reach, stance; age/layoff train-selected 2015-21) + Brier-fitted Elo->prob scale. **NEUTRAL-ORIENTATION LANDMINE: `ufc_fights` lists the WINNER as fighter1 — predictions are made on lexicographic fighter-id order or the label leaks.** Walk-forward (fit 2015-21, held-out 1,287 bouts 2022+): **winner FULL 61.3% / Brier 0.2297, calibration monotonic (70+ bucket won 81.2%); CORE (leakage-free) 59.6% / 0.2350; **DURATION family (P(finish) + measured finish-timing curve prices the whole rounds ladder AND the distance prop — books post whichever line is ~50/50 per fight): distance Brier 0.2344 vs 0.2421 base (the widest margin — purest expression, Kalshi `KXUFCDISTANCE`), over 0.5/1.5/2.5 all beat base — the flagship gate (NRFI's criterion) CLEARED on every line.** Static-stat layers (reach/stance/TD) use current career values — documented mild lookahead, reported separately. `compute_ufc_model.py` -> `ufc_model` snapshot table (DDL applied) via `.github/workflows/ufc-model.yml` (weekly Mon 11:00 UTC + dispatch backtest). Published model band is 60-65%, the close ~66% — winner stays on VETO duty as planned (sanity check: the snapshot makes Holloway 84% over McGregor off age+layoff+chin — the sentiment-fade archetype).

**Phase 3 (DONE July 2026): live wiring.** `handicapper_web._ufc_model_block` (cached `_ufc_snapshot` reader + layer math MIRRORED from `_lib/ufc_model.py` — keep in sync, Flask can't import kahla-scanner; verified to reproduce the engine: Holloway 84.2% vs 84.1%) attaches `dossier.ufc_model` on UFC dossiers: winner P + fair, per-fighter FightElo + human layer notes, and the DURATION family priced vs PMM's ACTUAL markets (distance via the `pmm_markets._classify_ufc_distance` sniff -> `pmm_data['ufc_distance']`, UNVERIFIED question shape — diag dump is ground truth; rounds via the PMM total block's posted line, priced only at lines the timing curve covers). Frontend: 'Fight IQ' OUR-NUMBER card. **Winner VETO live** (`UFC_MODEL_VETO_P=0.65`, needs `reliable` = both fighters >=5 rated bouts): a gated UFC ML steam pick the model rates <=35% demotes to a lean with `conflict_reason` — every UFC ML candidate records `ufc_model_p`/`ufc_model_agree`/`ufc_model_vetoed_pick`. **Paperlog shadows** (NEW VARIANTS — the dedup discriminator MUST know them, June-28-flood landmine): `'ufcdur'` (duration family: `market_type='distance'` side yes/no + rounds as `total`, flat 1u, `gates_cleared=false`, edge >= `UFC_DUR_EDGE_MIN_PP`=4pp) and `'ufcveto'` (vetoed ML counterfactuals). **The WHOLE UFC paperlog (ML + duration) is graded WEEKLY by `scripts/grade_ufc_paperlog.py`** (ufc-model.yml compute step) from `ufc_fights` (winner=fighter1 for MLs, draw→push; round+time for durations, 5R distance = 24:59) — extended to MLs July 13 2026 after the first-card review found the paperlog's UFC MLs had NO grader (the per-minute resolver grades UFC ML only in bot_picks) and had silently piled up pending. Earn-in (P4): promote distance/rounds to real picks (needs a bot_picks migration for `market_type='distance'` + resolver support) only after the shadow record prints. **P4 PROMOTED July 11 2026 by explicit user directive (NOT off the shadow record — "make picks, tiny stakes, entertainment, I'll manage the bank on UFC"):** the duration family now emits **real, loggable picks** on the Fight IQ card + games-list chips, NOT just shadows. `handicapper_web._ufc_dur_bet` turns each PMM-priced distance/rounds edge into a bet (maker bid entry, synthetic NO/UNDER inverted) in the `UFC_DUR_PICK_MIN_PP=2.0`–`UFC_DUR_PICK_MAX_PP=8.0` band (vs the 4pp uncapped shadow gate); flat 1u/low. **The 8pp EDGE CAP is the first-card lesson (July 13 2026): claimed edges >15pp went 0-for-3 — the third independent 'huge claimed edge = the market is right' finding (NRFI clamp, Diamond totals). Capped bets stay a labeled card tap, no chip/alert; shadows log uncapped so the cap stays measurable.** Real UFC picks also stamp `signal_blob.ufc_model_p/ufc_model_agree` (MLs) + `fight_iq/dur_edge_pp` (duration) so the model-agree review includes real money — the first-card review couldn't. Schema: migration `014_ufc_distance.sql` (**applied**) adds `'distance'` to the bot_picks market_type CHECK (side yes/no); **round O/U rides `market_type='total'`** (side over/under, line=round). `/api/handicapper/pick` accepts distance→yes/no. **GRADING IS MANUAL — the per-minute resolver skips ALL UFC non-ML (`unsupported`→stays pending), so distance + UFC-total picks never auto-grade; settle from the pending list** (the user opted into this). Frontend: Fight IQ card `logUfcDur(kind,side)` buttons + games-list chips (`_ufc:true` → shown whenever the fight is UPCOMING, no steam/model window gate since UFC duration isn't a steam bet); chip labels DIST/FINISH (distance) + RDS O/U (UFC total, via `d.sport==='UFC'` in `_pickChipLabel`). The `'ufcdur'` paperlog SHADOW keeps logging in parallel (unchanged, at the 4pp gate) as the forward validation dataset. Winner P stays veto/reference only (unchanged — it loses to the close, 60-65% vs ~66%).

### Diamond IQ — the MLB pitcher-spine rebuild (P1 done; P2 backtested July 2026 — verdict below)

**P1 (DONE): the pitcher history spine.** `mlb_pitcher_games` (DDL applied; 3,795 games / 32,494 pitcher rows, 2025-03-18 → present, starters = exactly 2× games) ← `scripts/ingest_mlb_pitchers.py` from the free MLB Stats API (schedule date-ranges → boxscore `pitchers` order; **first pitcher = the ACTUAL starter**, not the probable — so any model built on it backtests leakage-free; per-pitcher line: outs/BF/H/R/ER/K/BB/HR; spring training gameType S/E/A excluded per the preseason lesson). Probe-first via `.github/workflows/mlb-pitchers-ingest.yml` (probe/backfill/delta + daily 10:30 UTC delta). This spine is PERMANENT infrastructure — grading-grade pitcher history for any future model iteration.

**P2 (DONE July 2026 — honest verdict: the pitcher layer is REAL but v1 is not promotable).** `_lib/diamond_iq.py` (rolling per-pitcher FIP from actual game lines — K/BB/HR per out, IP-shrunk, 365d half-life — + team offense + bullpen runs-per-out, 60/40 starter/bullpen prevention blend → projected runs → margin) + `scripts/backtest_diamond_iq.py` (walk-forward replay, fit 2025 / eval 2026 n=1,318) + `diamond-iq-backtest.yml` (dispatch). Results: **FULL 54.0-54.6% / Brier 0.2486 (best variant, beats base) vs TEAM-ONLY 53.4-53.7% and NO-STARTER 53.1-53.6%** — the starter layer adds ~+1pp in every comparison (the signal the NHL goalie layer FAILED to show; consistent with baseball priors), but 54% is far from the market's ~57-58% and the delta is ~1 SE at this n. **Consequences:** nothing changes live — MLB sides stay exchange-anchored (the live v2 model already carries starter/bullpen/park/lineup layers and was graded OUT of sizing by live CLV); the MLB model's TOTALS role was subsequently BLACKLISTED too (July 4 2026 — see the O/U blacklist note). If Diamond IQ is pushed further, the levers are park factors, opponent-adjusted offense, and a better pitcher projection — not more team math. Engine validated on synthetic data (recovers a planted rotation signal at 60.5% while team-only is a coin flip).

### THE OPENER LANE + AUTO-BET (Aug 2 2026 — "the steam engine dies tonight")

**User directive (verbatim): "The steam engine dies tonight... turn Diamond IQ into an actual model, not a steam engine."** MLB betting decisions now come from the MODEL at LISTING TIME, not from movement. All in `app.py:_opener_pass` (rides the paperlog tick's leftover budget — naturally free evenings/overnight when tomorrow's lines post; the re-peg bot also runs 24/7, blackout removed):

- **Detection:** MLB games 6-72h out, one-shot per (game, market) via `pickbot_paperlog` opener rows (variant `'opener'` — in `_variant()` AND `_is_shadow_blob`, the June-28-flood rule); a game not yet PRICED retries every tick until it lists — with an in-memory per-game probe backoff (`_OPENER_PROBE_TS`, 420s, stamped only AFTER a completed build) so the unlisted tail doesn't eat the budget. Per-game rows persist IMMEDIATELY (`_opener_persist`) so a mid-tick crash loses nothing. **DEDUP LANDMINE (Aug 3 2026 — the "11 orders forever" all-nighter):** the market-row dedup key MUST be `(event_name, ET date)` (`_et_day`), never bare `event_name` — a multi-day window spans whole MLB series, and a bare-name dedup keeps only the EARLIEST row per matchup, silently deleting every next-day series game before the model runs (cands=0 → the lane bets NOTHING while lines sit posted). Same rule in `_gridiron_opener_pass` (168h NFL window = a week of name collisions). The bare-name dedup is correct ONLY in short windows (the 5h live paperlog loop) where a repeated name really is a dup row (gotcha #30). The tick's response JSON carries `opener`/`repeg` stats incl. a `gate` field naming every early return — read it from the scanner-poll job log (the workflow echoes the paperlog response body) before theorizing.
- **CAP KILLED + CAP-LATCH PROTOCOL (Aug 4 night — the watchdog's first catch):** `AUTOBET_MAX_BETS` is a 10000 sentinel (user: "kill that 40 cap") — the fences are per-bet (MASTER RULE $6, 60¢, the model). The cap machinery survives for a future re-cap and now counts **status='pending' ONLY** (settled games were holding slots — the lane sat 40/40 on dead bets while 14 would-bet Friday games latched). Protocol at every opener persist site: **BET BEFORE PERSIST, and a cap-blocked bet (`_autobet_execute` returns `"cap"`) NEVER persists** — the game retries when slots free. **WALL WATCHDOG** (`_opener_watchdog`, rides the tick): PMM lists 6+ future-day games but <6 standing future-day model bets → 🚨 urgent ping (quiet 10pm-7am AZ, 4h cooldown). Read-only by design — the user is not the alarm anymore. **PER-DAY HOLE check added Aug 10 2026** — the total-count rule missed the six-day spine outage entirely: the wall read a healthy 10 while all 10 sat on ONE date and every day behind it was empty. Now ANY listed ET date with ≥5 PMM-priced games and <2 standing bets trips it, whatever the total says.
- **STAKE + HARVEST — THE SELL ARM IS DEAD (killed Aug 19 2026, `_HARVEST_ENABLED=False`).** All lanes bet `_AUTOBET_CONTRACTS`=10, NRFI 5. Every position now rides to resolution; the 40 resting asks were swept with `/api/polymarket/reset-sells` and verified not to return. **The Aug 3 paired test answered over 161 fills and four target regimes: RIDING WINS, narrowly.** Lifetime the sell arm was **−$7.17** vs holding the same contracts, and every dollar of that sat in lanes that had already stopped selling (O/U −2.56, outs −2.13, unclassified props −2.41, K −0.70, hits −1.18). What was still running — ML only — was **+$1.81 over 91 sells**, about $0.13/day. Three things decided it: (1) every ladder rung sat BELOW its break-even touch bar at the lane's real 43.7% win rate / 45.5¢ entry (+40% touched 40.0 vs 44.1 needed, +50% 27.8 vs 36.0, **+65% 0/10 vs 25.7** — no loser ever touched the top rung, so it was winner-only by construction, and winners fill EVERY rung); (2) **a resting sell earns NO RENT — measured, not assumed**: on market-days where the harvest ask was the only order working, rent ran **$0.054/mkt-day vs $0.075 on markets with no ask at all** ($1.61 all-time).
