Cadence gates live in `scanner-poll.yml`'s `fill_ping` job (minute-modulo on the runner clock, BEFORE the curl). **Current cadence: pm-snapshot AND paperlog run every 1 min** (the check-fills + prime-alert curls were retired July 2026 — fill detection died with the Kalshi cutover, and the unlogged-bet alert rides the paperlog ping). The 1-min pings run SEQUENTIALLY in the one `fill_ping` job (~25-30s total), so they never truly stack on Vercel. **The real rule isn't "never 1-min" — it's "watch the Usage page."** If you add genuinely CPU-heavy (not I/O) per-minute work, check `Included Credit $X/$20` before/after; the $5 on-demand budget is the hard backstop. (The old "don't restore 1-min, that's the burn" warning was Hobby-specific — no longer applies on Pro.)
39. **Polymarket renamed the `sportsMarketType` (v1) slugs June 2026 → `pmm_markets._classify_market` must use a BLOCKLIST, not an allowlist.** Symptom: PMM data silently went to zero everywhere (pm-snapshot logged no PMM rows, the live dossier's `polymarket` block / make-take / exch_current fair anchor all empty) while Kalshi kept working and the SDK/auth/tag-slug were all fine. Root cause: `_classify_market` filtered main markets through an exact-match allowlist of the OLD short v1 slugs (`spreads`/`totals`/`moneyline`); Polymarket changed v1 to long form (`baseball_team_full_game_spread`, `baseball_team_full_game_total`, …) so every main market failed the allowlist → `counts {ml:0,spread:0,total:0}` even though `sportsMarketTypeV2` (`SPORTS_MARKET_TYPE_SPREAD`/`_TOTAL`) and the markets themselves were present. NRFI survived because it's sniffed by question text, not the v1 allowlist — that asymmetry (nrfi>0, ml/spread/total=0) is the fingerprint. Fix: take everything in the V2 bucket EXCEPT v1 substrings in `_VARIANT_MARKERS` (`first_five`, `first_inning`, `inning`, `*_half`, `quarter`, `period`, `player`) — a blocklist survives future renames. **Diagnosed via `/debug-pmm`** (public probe, no secrets): it constructs the client, tests `events.list` with real slugs, dumps the no-tag taxonomy, AND runs the full `pmm_markets.lookup` on a live game returning `diag.counts` + `diag.all_markets` (the `v1`/`v2` per market) — that's what exposed the rename. Keep `/debug-pmm` for the next time PMM goes quiet. If PMM data zeroes again: hit `/debug-pmm`, look at `lookup.diag.all_markets[].v1` — if the main spread/total markets carry a v1 not matched by the bucket logic, Polymarket renamed again; adjust `_VARIANT_MARKERS` (or the bucket) accordingly. **SECOND CASUALTY (found July 3): `pmm_markets.list_world_cup` had its own exact-match v1 allowlist (`drawable_outcome`/`moneyline`/`h2h`) that the June fix missed — the rename zeroed the ENTIRE World Cup feed (no `pm_snapshots`, no new WORLDCUP `markets`) from June 23 through the whole Round of 32, silently: the logger's `no_odds` skip counted every match as odds-less and nothing alerted. Fixed with `_wc_is_match_result` (variant-substring blocklist → result-marker substrings → V2-bucket fallback). Lesson: when a shared upstream schema changes, grep EVERY consumer of the field (`sportsMarketType`) — fixing the path you noticed is not fixing the bug. (The WC feed itself was removed entirely in the July 2026 soccer kill; the lesson stands.)**
39. **Exchange SPREAD fair must MIRROR-pair the sides (the "Reds -1.5 @ +292 phantom" — ROOT cause).** The cutover (June 19, commit 8962da6) made `_exch_two_sided_fair`/`_attach_exch_current` the sole SPR/TOT fair source (PIN's `_build_market_block` ran for months before, correctly). That new code picked ONE at-the-money line and stamped BOTH sides with it. **Right for TOTALS** (over/under share 8.5) but **WRONG for SPREADS**, whose sides are MIRROR lines (home -1.5 ↔ away +1.5). PMM lists each spread side at only its own line (home -1.5=50¢, away +1.5=50¢, **no** away -1.5 row), so the function fabricated the away side as the complement **at the home line** → a phantom **"Reds -1.5 @ 50%"** PMM never quoted. That phantom became a candidate and drove every downstream symptom: the ML/SPR exclusion side-flip ("sharp says Yankees → keep the only surviving SPR, which was the phantom Reds -1.5"), the +292 entry, and a fair that dodged the chalk filter. **Fix (live):** `_exch_two_sided_fair` mirror-pairs spreads — `home@L` devigged vs `away@(-L)`, **each side keeps its own line** (home -1.5, away +1.5); returns `((up_line, down_line), p_up, p_down)`; totals unchanged (shared line). **Lesson:** spread sides are opposite-sign lines — never assign them one shared line. Supporting guards that stay: the **SPR price band** is symmetric `-150..+186` (60¢-35¢, gotcha in the "Out-of-band SPR" note); `_suggest_picks` has a **line-consistency guard** (only trust a PMM quote whose line matches the fair line). **Related (same cutover):** the dossier freshness label reads `data_freshness.exch_latest_captured` (pm_snapshots, live ~2-min logger) as "exch Nm ago" — NOT the retired `odds_ingest_runs`/frozen `book_snapshots` ("cron 19h ago" was a dead-feed artifact, not an outage).
